**Series**: PO Tome 27

PREAMBULE PO 130-131 p. 3 PREAMBULE _PREAMBULE PREAMBULE Τοὺς περὶ εὐλογιῶν τοῦ ᾿Ιακὼδ ποιούμενος λόγους αὐτός, αὐτὸς τὴν παρὰ τοῦ κυρίου εὐλογίαν αἰτῶ. τίς γὰρ ἀνθρώπων ἱκανὸς διηγήσασθαι τὰ πνευματικῶς εἰρη- μένα, εἰ μὴ ἣ οὐράνιος σοφία συνεργεῖ τῷ λέγοντι; τίς δὲ ἑρμηνεῦσαι δύναται τὰ διὰ τῶν μακαρίων προφητῶν διὰ παραδολῶν αἰνιγματωδῶς εἰρημένα, εἰ μὴ αὐτὸς ὁ λόγος ἑαυτοῦ ἑρμηνεὺς γένηται; ὃς πάλαι μὲν < ἐν τῷ > Ἰσραὴλ ὡς λύχνος ἐν τῷ νόμῳ ὑπὸ τὸν μόδιον κρυπτόμενος ἵνα τοὺς ἐν νόμῳ διὰ τῆς περιτομῆς δικαιουμένους φω- τίσῃ, νυνὶ δὲ παρρησίᾳ ἐπὶ τοῦ ξύλου ὁ1 ὡς ἐν λυχνίᾳ ἑπτάμυξος λύχνος φαινόμε- ἐξ ἵνα τὰ μαχρὰν > ὑπάρχοντα ἔθνη πρὸς σωτηρίαν τὸ ἑαυτοῦ φῶς καλέσῃ. δῷ ον 8οο ϑοοχο φοροῦν ϑορῦ ον 85ογ δ ϑοβονοδοδο δυϑιωγογήἤεγϑοςο δῶν ϑοῦυ οὐο Ὀοδῶθδῃ ογοδοδοῦθθ ϑοϑϑφρηδρᾳ» ὁ δοϑοχονἤεγϑοςο δρόοῶον Ὀοῤῥροῦο. Τ᾽ J Վասն աւրծնութեանցն Յակոբայ առնելով բանս, ես զտեառն աւրծնութիւնսն խնդըեճ ։ Քանզի ἃ ոք ի ճարդկանե բաւական Է՝ պատճել վծողեկան ասացեալ գիրս, եթե ոչ նմա երկնական իմաստուԹիւնն գործակից լեցի ։ նաճ ճ ոք Ցեկնել կարող Է՝ վասացեալսն երանելի մարդարեիցն առակաւ եւ. աներեւոլժ բանեար, եթե ոչ նա ինքնին բանն գինքն ճեկնեսցե, որ յառաջ լԻսրաչեղի, որպես ճրագ ընդ աւրինաւքն, բրեւ ընդ գրուանաւ, Տածկեալ Էր, զի զարդարացեալսն աւրինացն Թլփատութեաճը ὁγϑοῶθο > οὗ > p. լրաաւորել` կարասցե τ Եւ ալժճ գաճարձակութեաճբ ի վերալ փայտի, իբրեւ. լաշտա- նակի եւնոստեան Ճրագ երեւեալ գի եւ լՏեռաւոր ազգս ի փրկութիւն եւ վիւր լոսն կոչել ΠΩ է Τοῖς περὶ εὐλογιῶν τοῦ ᾿Ιακὼδ ποιούμενος λόγους αὐτός, αὐτὸς τὴν παρὰ τοῦ κυρίου εὐλογίαν αἰτῶ. τίς γὰρ ἀνθρώπων ἱκανὸς διηγήσασθαι τὰ πνευματικῶς εἰρη- μένα, εἰ μὴ ἣ οὐράνιος σοφία συνεργεῖ τῷ λέγοντι; τίς δὲ ἑρμηνεῦσαι δύναται τὰ διὰ τῶν μακαρίων προφητῶν διὰ παραδολῶν αἰνιγματωδῶς εἰρημένα, εἰ μὴ αὐτὸς ὁ λόγος ἑαυτοῦ ἑρμηνεὺς γένηται; ὃς πάλαι μὲν < ἐν τῷ > Ἰσραὴλ ὡς λύχνος ἐν τῷ νόμῳ ὑπὸ τὸν μόδιον κρυπτόμενος ἵνα τοὺς ἐν νόμῳ διὰ τῆς περιτομῆς δικαιουμένους φω- τίσῃ, νυνὶ δὲ παρρησίᾳ ἐπὶ τοῦ ξύλου ὁ1 ὡς ἐν λυχνίᾳ ἑπτάμυξος λύχνος φαινόμε- ἐξ ἵνα τὰ μαχρὰν > ὑπάρχοντα ἔθνη πρὸς σωτηρίαν τὸ ἑαυτοῦ φῶς καλέσῃ. δῷ ον 8οο ϑοοχο φοροῦν ϑορῦ ον 85ογ δ ϑοβονοδοδο δυϑιωγογήἤεγϑοςο δῶν ϑοῦυ οὐο Ὀοδῶθδῃ ογοδοδοῦθθ ϑοϑϑφρηδρᾳ» ὁ δοϑοχονἤεγϑοςο δρόοῶον Ὀοῤῥροῦο. At the moment when, upon the blessings of Jacob, I undertake to discourse (1), I myself ask for the blessing (2) that comes to us from the Lord (3). Who, indeed, among men is capable of narrating (4) what has been said in a spiritual sense, if the heavenly Wisdom does not collaborate with him while he speaks? And (5) who can interpret what has been said by the blessed prophets by means of parables, in veiled words, if the Word himself does not make himself his own interpreter? (This Word) who, in the ancient days first, in Israel, like a lamp, in the time of the Law, was hidden under a bushel in order to illuminate those who were, in the time of the Law, justified by means of circumcision; then (Word who), in the time in which we are, in a language that says everything, appears on the wood, like a seven-branched lamp on a candlestick, in order to call to salvation, to his own light, even to the distant nations (6).

Παρέστω τοίνυν ἡμῖν αὐτὸς ὁ λόγος ἑρμηνεὺς τῶν ἑαυτοῦ μυστηρίων γινόμενος, ἵνα καὶ τὰ τοῦ Ἰωσὴφ ὁράματα τοῖς πιστεύουσιν φανερῶσαι δυνηθῇ. οὐδὲ γὰρ μάτην τοῦτον ὁραματιστὴν ἐκάλουν οἱ ἀδελφοί, εἰ μὴ δυνάμει προεώρων τὸν ἐν αὐτῷ πολιτευό- ΡΟ p. μενον λόγον. « εἶπον γὰρ πρὸς ἑαυτοὺς λέγοντες. ἰδοὺ ὁ ὁραματιστὴς ἔρχεται" δεῦτε καὶ ἀποχτείνωμεν αὐτόν, καὶ ἴδωμεν τί ἔσται τὰ ὁράματα αὐτοῦ ». τίνα δὲ ἦν τὰ τούτου ὁράματα, υἱοὶ Ιακώδ, εἴπατε. ἐθεώρει, φησίν, δράγματα ἐν πεδίῳ δεσμούμενα
(7) May he therefore assist us himself, the Word, making himself the interpreter of his own mysteries, so that he may also put his power into making the visions of Joseph clear to the believers. And, indeed, it is not without reason that his brothers called him a visionary, since they did not see in advance the Word, by power, making his dwelling in him. They said indeed, speaking to themselves: "Here comes the visionary! Come, let us kill him, and let us see what will become of his visions!" But what were his visions, sons of Jacob, tell me? -
καὶ τὸ ἑαυτοῦ δράγμα ὀρθούμενον, τὰ δὲ ἡμέτερα πεσόντα᾽ ἐπιστραφέντα δὲ προσεκύνουν τὸ τοῦ Ἰωσὴφ δράγμα. τί ἐν τούτῳ ἠδίκησεν ᾿Ιωσήφ, εἰ τὸ μέλλον ἔσεσθαι ἀπήγγειλεν ὑμῖν; τί παραζηλοῦτε καὶ μισεῖτε τὸν δίκαιον, εἰ θεὸς τούτῳ τὰ ἑαυτοῦ μυστήρια ἀπεκάλυψεν καὶ τὰ ἐπ’ ἐσχάτων γινόμενα δι᾽ ὁραμάτων ἐφανέρωσεν; τί ἐλυπήθητε ἰδόντες χιτῶνα ποικίλον ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ, εἰ ὁ δίκαιος πατὴρ τοῦτον παρὰ πάντας ἀγαπήσας ἐτίμησεν καὶ ποιμένα ποιμένων εἰς ἐπίσκεψιν ὑμῖν ἀπέστειλεν καὶ μάρτυρα πιστὸν ἐν κόσμῳ προήγαγεν καὶ δράγμα γήρως ὡς ἀπαρχὴν ἅγιον πρωτότοχον νεχρῶν ἀνέστησεν; τί ἀγαναχτεῖτε, εἰ ἥλιος καὶ σελήνη καὶ ἕνδεκα ἀστέρες προσεκύνουν αὐτόν, οἱ πάλαι προτυπούμενοι; οὐδὲ Ἰακὼδ καὶ ῬῬαχὴλ ἥλιος καὶ σελήνη ἐχρημάτιζεν, ἀλλ᾽ οὐδὲ γὰρ οὕτω γεγένηται. ἡ μὲν γὰρ Ῥαχὴλ ἤδη τεθνεῶσα ἐν τῷ ἱπποδρόμῳ ἐτέθαπτο, ὁ δὲ ᾿Ιωσὴφ συναντήσας τῷ πατρὶ αὑτοῦ, πεσὼν προσεκύνησεν, ἵνα ὁ τύπος τηρηθῇ καὶ ἡ Ὑ J οδοῦο οὥσδηςο οὐδοῦ Q> δῶν > ϑονωβοῶθ > δῶσ 8ῶ.»- ϑοὺ միալն կերպարանեալք. այլ ոչ եթե Ցակոբ եւ Ռաքել արեգակն եւ լուսին
"He was contemplating, he says, in a plain, bound sheaves; and his own sheaf stood upright, but ours had fallen; then having turned toward it, they worshipped the sheaf of Joseph." What rights, in this instance, did Joseph violate, if he announced to you there what was to be? Why do you envy and hate the righteous one, if God revealed to him his own mysteries, and if he made clear by visions the events of the end of times? Why your saddened faces at the sight of a tunic embroidered on his back, if the Just Father (8), in his love preferring him to all, honored him, and sent you to visit a Shepherd of shepherds, and produced in the world a faithful witness, and, a sheaf of old age (9), like firstfruits, made a holy Firstborn rise from the dead? Why be indignant if the sun and the moon and eleven stars worshipped him? They are there, from ancient times, as prefigurers (10). And neither was Jacob called (11) "Sun," nor Rachel, "Moon." For indeed, things did not happen that way. Rachel, she, indeed, already dead, lay buried in the hippodrome. As for Joseph, when he had gone to meet his father, having prostrated himself, he worshipped so that the value of the type would be well kept and that the Scripture would appear to be telling the truth (12). Where then is fulfilled what was
եին. եւ ոչ բնաւ ալդ այդպես եղեալ Է, քանզի Ռաքել վաղուց մեռեալ` ասպարիսին թաղեալ կալը ։ Իսկ Յովսեփայ պատածեալ աւրն՝ յառաջ նա անկեալ երկիր ե- պագ, զի աւըինակն պաճեսցի, եւ դիրք արդարացեալ երեւեսցի: Արդ ճեր կատարի ասացեալն. արդեւք եկեալ ես եւ ճալը քո եւ եղբարք քո էրկիր պազցութ քեզ ի վերալ γραφὴ ἀλχηθεύουσα φανῇ. ποῦ οὖν πληροῦται τὸ εἰρημένον, τὸ « ἄρά γε ἐλθόντες ἐγὼ καὶ ἡ μήτηρ σου καὶ οἱ ἀδελφοί σου προσκυνήσωμέν σοι ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς », ἀλλ᾽ ἢ ὅτε οἱ μακάριοι ἀπόστολοι ἅμα ᾿Ιωσὴφ καὶ Μαρίᾳ ἐλθόντες ἐν τῷ ὄρει τῶν ἐλαιῶν προσεκύνησαν τὸν Χριστόν; ᾿Ιούδας αὐτῶν οὐχ ἦν. οὐδὲ γὰρ ὁ Ἰωσὴφ αὐτὸς Χριστὸς ἦν, ἵνα ἐν Αἰγύπτῳ τοῦτο γεγενῆσθαι νομισθῇ, ἀλλὰ ἦν τύπος τοῦ μέλλοντος παραδεικνυμένου διὰ Ἰωσήφ᾽ παρέστω τοίνυν ὁ ἀληθινὸς καὶ ἐπουράνιος Ιωσὴφ ἑρμηνεύς, ἵνα τὰ δι᾽ αὐτοῦ κηρυχθέντα ὑπὸ τῶν μακαρίων προφητῶν αὐτὸς πάλιν ἑρμηνεύων φανῇ᾽ οὐδὲ γὰρ παρεσιώπησεν ἡ γραφὴ ἐπὶ τοῦ Ἰωσήφ, ἀλλὰ ἑρμηνέα τοῦτον ἀπέφηνεν᾽ αὐτὸς γὰρ ἦν ἑρμηνεὺς τῶν τοῦ πατρὸς ἀποχρύφων μυστηρίων. καὶ γὰρ ἡ γραφὴ λέγει" « αὐτοὶ δὲ οὐκ ἔγνωσαν ὅτι ἀκούει » αὐτῶν « Ἰωσήφ᾽ ὁ γὰρ ἑρμηνεὺς ἀνὰ μέσον αὐτῶν ἦν ». ϑοϑοῦν T b δοῶδοφοσφο δ56 οδο δῦ φῦ θοβδδδο δυδϑοῶσγς»» J ὅσης» οδωδδοδ οὗ ὁφοδσορδοῦ οδο. > ϑω- οϑο-ϑοςῦθον τοὺ @> ὅϑοῦδο ϑοδῦο @>
said, namely: "Shall we then have to come, I and your mother and your brothers, to worship you face to the ground?" if not when (13) the blessed Apostles, together with Joseph and Mary, once arrived on the Mount of Olives, worshipped Christ? Judas was not with them (14). And Joseph, indeed, was not Christ in person, to the point that one would go to believe that this (prophetic vision) was realized in Egypt, but he was the figure of the (Christ) to come, represented by Joseph (15). (16) May he come then, the true and heavenly Joseph, as interpreter, so that, what, through his intermediary, was announced by the blessed Prophets, He himself, again, by interpreting it, may make himself seen! (17) And, indeed, the Scripture did not (on this) keep silence in the history of Joseph, but it made us see it well (18), Him (i.e., the heavenly Joseph), as interpreter. He himself indeed was the interpreter of the hidden mysteries of the Father. And, indeed, the Scripture says: "As for them, they did not perceive (19) that Joseph understood them, for the interpreter was an intermediary between them."
եկեալ ի լերինն Ճիքենեաց, երկիր պաղին Քրիստոսի, քանզի Ցուղալ չեր ընդ նոսա, եւ ոչ Յուսեփ ինքնին Քրիստոս Եր, վի չԵգւպտոսի այս եղեալ Համարեսցի ։ Ալ Եր նմանութիւն լինելոց իրացն, ռր առաջ երեւեցաւ Ցովսեփաւ ։ Եկեսցե ի մեջ այժմ Ճշմարիտ եւ երկնաւոր ճեկնիչն, զի որ նովաւն քարողեցաւ երանելի մարգարեիցն, նա դարձեալ մեկնեսցե ։ Այլ եւ ոչ ղանց արարին դիրք զՅովսեփաւ՝ թարգման ղնա ասելով, քանդի նա Էր արդման Հաւր ծածուկ խորրդոցն. եւ դիրք ասեն` եթե նոքա ոչ գիտելն՞ երե լսեր նոցա Ցովսեփ, քանգի Pարզճան ի մեջ նոցա կայր: Παρέστω τοίνυν ἡμῖν αὐτὸς ὁ λόγος U2 τῶν ἑαυτοῦ μυστηρίων γινόμενος, ἵνα καὶ τὰ τοῦ Ἰωσὴφ ὁράματα τοῖς πιστεύουσιν φανερῶσαι δυνηθῇ. οὐδὲ γὰρ μάτην τοῦτον ὁραματιστὴν ἐκάλουν οἱ ἀδελφοί, εἰ μὴ δυνάμει προεώρων τὸν ἐν αὐτῷ πολιτευό- μενον λόγον. « εἶπον γὰρ πρὸς ἑαυτοὺς λέγοντες. ἰδοὺ ὁ ὁραματιστὴς ἔρχεται" δεῦτε καὶ ἀποκτείνωμεν αὐτόν, καὶ ἴδωμεν τί ἔσται τὰ ὁράματα αὐτοῦ ». τίνα δὲ ἦν τὰ τούτου ὁράματα, υἱοὶ Ἰακώδ, εἴπατε. ἐθεώρει, φησίν, δράγματα ἐν πεδίῳ δεσμούμενα καὶ τὸ ἑαυτοῦ δράγμα ὀρθούμενον, τὰ δὲ ἡμέτερα πεσόντα" ἐπιστραφέντα δὲ προσεκύνουν τὸ τοῦ Ἰωσὴφ δράγμα. τί ἐν τούτῳ ἠδίκησεν Ἰωσήφ, εἰ τὸ μέλλον ἔσεσθαι ἀπήγγειλεν ὑμῖν; τί παραζηλοῦτε καὶ μισεῖτε τὸν δίκαιον, εἰ θεὸς τούτῳ τὰ ἑαυτοῦ μυστήρια ἀπεκάλυψεν. καὶ τὰ ἐπ᾽ ἐσχάτων γινόμενα δι᾽ ὁραμάτων ἐφανέρωσεν; τί ἐλυπήθητε ἰδόντες χιτῶνα ποικίλον ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ, εἰ ὁ δίκαιος πατὴρ τοῦτον παρὰ πάντας ἀγαπήσας ἐτίμησεν καὶ ποιμένα ποιμένων εἰς ἐπίσκεψιν ὑμῖν ἀπέστειλεν καὶ μάρτυρα πιστὸν ἐν κόσμῳ προήγαγεν καὶ δράγμα γήρως ὡς ἀπαρχὴν ἅγιον πρωτότοκον νεχρῶν ἀνέστησεν; τί ἀγανακτεῖτε, εἰ ἥλιος καὶ σελήνη καὶ ἕνδεκα ἀστέρες προσεκύνουν αὐτόν, οἱ πάλαι προτυπούμενοι; οὐδὲ ᾿Ιακὼδ καὶ Ραχὴλ ἥλιος καὶ σελήνη ἐχρημάτιζεν, ἀλλ᾽ οὐδὲ γὰρ οὕτω γεγένηται. ἣ μὲν γὰρ Ραχὴλ ἤδη τεθνεῶσα ἐν τῷ ἱπποδρόμῳ ἐτέθαπτο, ὁ δὲ ᾿Ιωσὴφ συναντήσας τῷ πατρὶ αὑτοῦ, πεσὼν προσεκύνησεν, ἵνα ὁ τύπος τηρηθῇ καὶ ἧ T a, J οὐδῶδ > δῶσ > ϑονωβοῶθ τοῦ δ 8ῶ.- ϑοὺ ςουϑοῶθηφρο միալն կերպարանեալք. այլ ոչ եթե Ցակոբ եւ Ռաքել արեգակն եւ լուսին եին. եւ ոչ բնաւ ալդ այդպես եղեալ Է, քանզի Ռաքել վաղուց մեռեալ` ասպարիսին քաղեալ կայր ։ Իսկ Յովսեփայ պատածեալ Հաւրե՝ յառաջ նա անկեալ երկիր ե- պագ, զի աւըինակն պաՏեսցի, եւ դիրք արդարացեալ`երեւեսցին: Արդ ճեր կատարի ասացեալն. արդեւք եկեալ ՝ ես եւ ճալը բո եւ եղբարք բո երկիր պազցութ քեզ ի վերլ
said, namely: "Shall we then have to come, I and your mother and your brothers, to worship you face to the ground?" if not when (13) the blessed Apostles, together with Joseph and Mary, once arrived on the Mount of Olives, worshipped Christ? Judas was not with them (14). And Joseph, indeed, was not Christ in person, to the point that one would go to believe that this (prophetic vision) was realized in Egypt, but he was the figure of the (Christ) to come, represented by Joseph (15). (16) May he come then, the true and heavenly Joseph, as interpreter, so that, what, through his intermediary, was announced by the blessed Prophets, He himself, again, by interpreting it, may make himself seen! (17) And, indeed, the Scripture did not (on this) keep silence in the history of Joseph, but it made us see it well (18), Him (i.e., the heavenly Joseph), as interpreter. He himself indeed was the interpreter of the hidden mysteries of the Father. And, indeed, the Scripture says: "As for them, they did not perceive (19) that Joseph understood them, for the interpreter was an intermediary between them."
γραφὴ ἀληθεύουσα φανῇ. ποῦ οὖν πληροῦται τὸ εἰρημένον, τὸ « ἄρά γε ἐλθόντες ἐγὼ καὶ ἡ μήτηρ σου καὶ οἱ ἀδελφοί σου προσκυνήσωμέν σοι ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς », ἀλλ᾽ ἢ ὅτε οἱ μακάριοι ἀπόστολοι ἅμα Ἰωσὴφ καὶ Μαρίᾳ ἐλθόντες ἐν τῷ ὄρει τῶν ἐλαιῶν προσεκύνησαν τὸν Χριστόν; ᾿Ιούδας μετ᾽ αὐτῶν οὐκ ἦν. οὐδὲ γὰρ ὁ Ἰωσὴφ αὐτὸς Χριστὸς ἦν, ἵνα ἐν Αἰγύπτῳ τοῦτο γεγενῆσθαι νομισθῇ, ἀλλὰ ἦν τύπος τοῦ μέλλοντος παραδεικνυμένου διὰ Ἰωσήφ᾽ παρέστω τοίνυν ὁ ἀληθινὸς καὶ ἐπουράνιος Ιωσὴφ ἑρμηνεύς, ἵνα τὰ δι᾽ αὐτοῦ κηρυχθέντα ὑπὸ τῶν μακαρίων προφητῶν αὐτὸς πάλιν ἑρμηνεύων φανῇ" οὐδὲ γὰρ παρεσιώπησεν ἣ γραφὴ ἐπὶ τοῦ Ἰωσήφ, ἀλλὰ ἑρμηνέα τοῦτον ἀπέφηνεν' αὐτὸς γὰρ ἦν ἑρμηνεὺς τῶν τοῦ πατρὸς ἀποχρύφων μυστηρίων. καὶ γὰρ ἡ γραφὴ λέγει" « αὐτοὶ δὲ οὐκ ἔγνωσαν ὅτι ἀκούει » αὐτῶν « Ἰωσήφ᾽ ὁ γὰρ ἑρμηνεὺς ἀνὰ μέσον αὐτῶν ἦν ». ϑοϑοῦ T b > δυδϑοώογς»» J ὅσης» οὗ οδο. > @> @> @> οδο δοόσῶδο ϑωβοφοφοῦο @> @> ογοῦσ ϑοδὺ οὐχ θοδοο θοῦ δοϑωσοῦστου οὗ βυθϑοΘοθο > δοδδϑοῶῥοδοαρο ϑορῶ δυϑωσρῦσδωρσδ ὑὥσϑὺ δυῶν ϑοβόφοοῦ σῶν δοδϑϑοῶῥῤρονᾳ» δοϑωϑονδῶωδοο» T ἃ @> ον ϑοοοῶϑρυ ϑωθοῦ p. երկըի, եթե ոչ լալնժամ լորժամ երանելի առաքեալքն գանդերձ Յովսեվաւ եւ Մարեճաւ եկեալ ի լերինն Ճիթենեաց, երկիր պաղին Քրիստոսի, քանզի Ցուղալ չեր ընդ նոսա, եւ ոչ Յուսեփ ինքնին Քրիստոս Եր, վի չեգւպտոսի այս եղեալ Համարեսցի ։ Ալ Եր նմանութիւն լինելոց իրացն, ռը առաջ երեւեցաւ Յովսեփաւ Եկեսցե ի մեջ այժմ Ճշմարիտ եւ երկնաւոր ճեկնիչն, զի որ նովաւն քարողեցաւ երանելի մարգարեիցն, նա դարձեալ մեկնեսցե ։ Այլ եւ ոչ դանց արարին դիրք զճովսեփամ՝ թարգման ղնա ասելով, քանդի նա Էր Թարդման Հաւր ծածուկ խորՏրդոցն. եւ գիրք ասեն` եթե Tոքա ոչ դիտեին՝ եքե լսեր նոցա Ցովսեվ, քանզի թարգման ի δε նոցա կալը» Τί ὠφέλησαν οἱ ἀδελφοὶ ἔριφον τότε ἀποχτείναντες καὶ τὸν ἑαυτῶν πατέρα ϑυϑοῦ ϑοϑλὼ
What did the brothers gain by having then killed a kid and deceived their father (20)? For behold, Joseph, in Egypt, received the title (21) of king. Why have you
Զինչ աւդտեցան եղբարքն ղուլն ալծից լալնժամ սպանանել, լի դճալրն իւրեանց πλανήσαντες; ἰδοὺ γὰρ ὁ Ἰωσὴφ ἐν βασιλεὺς ἐχρημάτισεν. τί ἐμολύνατε τὸν χιτῶνα τὸν ποικίλον τῷ αἵματι τοῦ ἐρίφου ψευδόμενοι καὶ παρερμηνεύοντες τὰ ὑφ᾽ ὑμῶν γεγενημένα; ὁ δὲ πατὴρ χιτῶνα υἱοῦ τοῦτον εἶναι ὡμολόγει μεθ᾽ ὑμῶν καὶ ἔφερεν τὸ γεγενημένον. σὺ δὲ πωλεῖς, Ἰούδα, τὸν ᾿Ιωσὴφ ἐπιθυμίᾳ κόσμου κρατούμενος. οἱ δὲ ἀλλόφυλοι Ἰσμαηλῖται τοῦτον ἀγοράζουσιν, ἵνα εἰς Αἴγυπτον ἀχθῇ κἀκεῖ δέσμιος γένηται καὶ ὁράματα δι᾿αὐτοῦ ἀποκαλυφθῇ καὶ ὑπὸ Φαραῶ ζητηθῇ καὶ βασιλεὺς Αἰγύπτου κατασταθῇ καὶ τὸν δεύτερον θρόνον πισ τευθῇ καὶ σῖτος πολὺς δι᾽ αὐτοῦ συναχθῇ καὶ εἰς ἀποθήκην τηρηθῇ καὶ ἡ ἑδδομὰς ἐτῶν πληρωθῇ καὶ λαὸς λιμώττων τραφῇ καὶ ὑπὸ ἀδελφῶν προσχυνηθῇ καὶ πατὴρ ὑπ᾽ αὐτῶν ὁμολογηθῇ. οδοϑρὺ οσυχδο > οὐδοῦ 235560Ե δῶν ϑο3δρυ- ογήσγοδ
soiled the tunic, the embroidered tunic! with the blood of this kid (22), lying and misinterpreting the events of which you were, yourselves, the authors (23)? The father, he, was in agreement with you to recognize that it was indeed the tunic of a son and endured (with pain) the event (24). And you, Judah, you sell Joseph, defeated as you are by "the concupiscence of the world." And the foreign Ishmaelites buy him so that in Egypt he may be led and that there he may be put in irons and that, of visions, the hidden meaning may be, by him, unveiled and that he may be, by the Pharaoh, summoned and that he may be instituted King of Egypt and sit, in testimony of trust, on the second throne (25); and that wheat, in abundance, may be by him gathered and kept in storage; and that the week of years may be fulfilled; and that the starving people may be fed and that he may be, by his brothers, worshipped, and father, by them, recognized (26).

soiled the tunic, the embroidered tunic! with the blood of this kid (22), lying and misinterpreting the events of which you were, yourselves, the authors (23)? (28), our attention once firmly fixed, let us see if the blessings pronounced by Jacob truly went, as blessings, upon the sons of Israel. For we find the contrary, namely: on one hand, blessings which there (in Egypt or in the Bible) are indeed such by their formulation; on the other hand, proof of their (the sons of Israel's) guilt, and, presented in their persons, a very ample prophecy which was realized in the lineage of those who sprouted from their stock. So much so that the blessed Jacob was speaking, it is true, to his own sons, as if it were a matter of the present, but what he said happened in the latter times. And, on one hand, beforehand, he distinguished the blessings by means of which these things (to come) were prophesied; on the other hand, he gave them to the sons of Israel, while proving their transgressions, and he threatened them, withdrawing from such actions (29). How, then, shall I not glorify the Heavenly King, He who, by making use of prophets, staged (27) His own mysteries? (28), our attention once firmly fixed, let us see if the blessings pronounced by Jacob truly went, as blessings, upon the sons of Israel. For we find the contrary, namely: on one hand, blessings which there (in Egypt or in the Bible) are indeed such by their formulation; on the other hand, proof of their (the sons of Israel's) guilt, and, presented in their persons, a very ample prophecy which was realized in the lineage of those who sprouted from their stock. So much so that the blessed Jacob was speaking, it is true, to his own sons, as if it were a matter of the present, but what he said happened in the latter times. And, on one hand, beforehand, he distinguished the blessings by means of which these things (to come) were prophesied; on the other hand, he gave them to the sons of Israel, while proving their transgressions, and he threatened them, withdrawing from such actions (29).

in advance the wicked calculation that would come into his heart, did not bless him [in his turn], but, in the strength of the term, by a prophecy, he reprimanded him. Moreover, that it is indeed so, Scripture itself, once opened, will teach us more clearly what was said previously (30). [p. 12] {#po.27.p12}
προειδώς που τὸν < ἐν > τῇ καρδίᾳ πονηρὸν λογισμόν, τοῦτον οὐκ αὖ ηὐλόγησεν, ἀλλά, δυνάμει ῥητῶν, διὰ προφητείας ἐπέπληξεν. ὅτι δὲ οὕτως ἔχει, αὐτὴ ἡ γραφὴ ἀνα- πτυχθεῖσα σαφέστερον ἡμᾶς διδάξει τὰ προειρημένα. λέγει γὰρ οὕτως" « καὶ ἐκάλε- σεν Ἰσαὰκ τὸν υἱὸν αὐτοῦ τὸν πρεσθύτερον καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ" λάθε τὸ σκεῦός σου, τήν τε φαρέτραν καὶ τὸ τόξον καὶ ἔξελθε εἰς τὸ πεδίον καὶ θήρευσόν μοι θήραν, καὶ ποίησόν μοι ἐδέσματα ὡς φιλῶ ἐγώ" καὶ ἔνεγκέ μοι, ἵνα φάγω, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ σε ἡ ψυχή μου πρὸ τοῦ ἀποθανεῖν με ». τὸ μὲν οὖν ἐντέλλεσθαι τὸν προφήτην τροφὴν αἰτοῦντα παρὰ
to me, that I may eat it, so that my soul may bless you before I die.' The fact, therefore, that, on one hand, the prophet, asking Esau for food, does so by giving him an order (31), signifies the call that the Word makes the first people hear when he asks them for the fruit of the works of justice, (justice) which was considered as food for the Father (32). For these words: 'Go out into the plain and kill me some game' designate life in the world. The fact, on the other hand, of saying to him: 'Take your quiver and your bow' shows that the people were to, claiming (33) their personal glory, not
τοῦ Ἡσαῦ σημαίνει τὴν διὰ τοῦ λόγου κλῆσιν τοῦ προτέρου λαοῦ γεγενημένην, ὡς ἀπαιτεῖ καρπὸν ἔργων δικαιοσύνης παρ᾽ αὐτοῦ, ἥτις ἦν ὡς τροφὴ τῷ πατρὶ λογιζομένη. τὸ γὰρ « ἔξελθε εἰς τὸ πεδίον καὶ θήρευσόν μοι θήραν » τὴν ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ ἀναστροφὴν σημαίνει. τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν πρὸς αὐτόν, « λάθε τὴν φαρέτραν καὶ τὸ τόξον σου» δηλοῖ, ὅτι ἔμελλεν ὁ λαὸς ἰδίας δόξης ἀντιποιούμενος μὴ πίστει δικαιοῦσθαι, ἀλλ᾽ ἐν πολέμῳ καὶ ὁθο-ὦ φῦ δῶν οὥσϑοςο ΕΚ ΌΝΝ σγβο)ϑγφρονο
be justified by faith, but, glorying in war and the sword, ask for a king (who will be) their tyrant (34); as Moses, too, said to them: '... and the sword is that in which you glory' (35). Moreover, we find that the blessed Isaac also acted similarly, and that Jacob, his younger son, he blessed and granted all favors because of Christ who, according to the flesh, was to be begotten of him. But Esau, the type-bearer of the first people, (Isaac), knowing in some way
in advance the wicked calculation that would come into his heart, did not bless him [in his turn], but, in the strength of the term, by a prophecy, he reprimanded him. Moreover, that it is indeed so, Scripture itself, once opened, will teach us more clearly what was said previously (30). Thus, indeed, it speaks: "And Isaac called his elder son and said to him: 'Take your equipment: and your quiver and your bow, and go out into the plain, and kill me some game, and make me a good dish, as I love it, and bring it to me, that I may eat it, so that my soul may bless you before I die.' The fact, therefore, that, on one hand, the prophet, asking Esau for food, does so by giving him an order (31), signifies the call that the Word makes the first people hear when he asks them for the fruit of the works of justice, (justice) which was considered as food for the Father (32). For these words: 'Go out into the plain and kill me some game' designate life in the world. The fact, on the other hand, of saying to him: 'Take your quiver and your bow' shows that the people were to, claiming (33) their personal glory, not


[p. 13] {#po.27.p13}
ըանա ժողովրդեանն ունի, եւ զսրտին զչար խորգուրդսն իմացեալ ՝ ոչ աւրգնեաց, ալլ զաւրութեաճը ճառւիցն ճարգարեութեամը տանջեաց ։ Եւ վասն զե ալդ այդպես Է՝ ինջեին իսկ գիրը ի եջ եկեալ լալտնագոլն յուցցեն դլառաջակաան ε Բանգի աս ապես. եւ կոչ եայ, ասե, Իսաակ զորդին իւր զանդրանիկ, եւ ասաց նճա. առ. վկազճած բո, զաղեղն եւ զկապարճս, եւ ել ի դաշտ, եւ որսա ինճ որս, եւ արա ինձ կերակուր որպես եւ սիրեմ ես, եւ բեր ինճ, վի կերայց եւ աւրգնեսցե գքեզ անձն իճ առաջ քան զճեռանել ։ Եւ Հրաման տալ ճարդարեին Եսաւայ, եւ խնդրել ի նճանե կերակուր` նշանակե ղկոչումն բանիւն առաջին ժողովրդեանն, որ խնդրե պտուղ գործոց արդարունեան ի նճանե՝ որ Եր իբըեւ զկերակուր Տողւոչ գամարեալ ε Եւ զի ասաց՝ թե ել ի դաշտ եւ որսա ինձ որս, զգնացս լաշխարճի ժողովրդեանն նշանակե Ն. ասելն առ. նա` Աե առ զաղեղն եւ զկապարճս քո, ցուցանե եթե գանդերձեալ Եր be justified by faith, but, glorying in war and the sword, ask for a king (who will be) their tyrant (34); as Moses, too, said to them: '... and the sword is that in which you glory' (35).

որսա ինձ որս, զգնացս լաշխարճի ժողովրդեանն նշանակե ։ Եւ վասելն առ նա` թե առ զաղեղն եւ դկապարճս քո, ցուցանե եթե Հանդերձեալ Եր ժողովուրդն զիւր փառսն սիրել, եւ ոչ գաւատովք արդարանալ, ալլ պատերազմաւ


[p. 15] {#po.27.p15}
δῶν ὈοΟθϑιηδωβοδοο»» τοῦ ϑοῦχαροῦο ϑωῦδο8 @> δοδῳοδοϑοφροῖο δοϑογῶβ,δοα. δοογοῶρο ϑωδοϑὸδ ϑοσγοῶϑοςο ϑυῦχᾳ» ϑοῦςοῦ եւ սրով պարծել, եւ վրնդդիմադարձ թագաւորն իւր ընտրել, որպես եւ Մովսեսն իսկ ասաց առ նոսա՝ եթե սուր պարծանք քո: « Εἶπεν δὲ πρὸς Ἰακὼδ τὸν υἱὸν αὐτῆς τὸν νεώτερον᾽ ἰδοὺ ἐγὼ ἤκουσα τοῦ πατρός σου λαλοῦντος πρὸς Ἡσαῦ τὸν ἀδελφόν σου καὶ λέγοντος" ἔνεγκέ μοι θήραν καὶ ποίησόν μοι ἐδέσματα καὶ φαγὼν εὐλογήσω σε ἐναντίον κυρίου. νῦν οὖν ἄκουσόν μου τέκνον τῆς φωνῆς καὶ πορευθεὶς εἰς τὰ πρόδθατα λάδε μοι ἐκεῖθεν δύο ἐρίφους ἁπαλοὺς καὶ καλοὺς καὶ ποιήσω αὐτοὺς ἐδέσματα τῷ πατρί σου ὡς φιλεῖ καὶ εἰσοίσεις τῷ πατρί σου καὶ φάγεται, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ < σε > ὁ πατήρ σου πρὸ τοῦ ἀποθανεῖν αὐτόν ». © Ορδρδὺ ϑῶβοδθυ. ϑοῦϑωθῦ T a ϑοδοῦ»ο. οόοτοῦ ϑοδοςοοῶρ ὃ δοφοοῶο φῦ ϑοήϑοδ ©> ϑο > ὃ56 θοδοϑῃ δοῦ- > > ϑωϑρ)σῶσδ φῦ > ϑοϑοδο > ϑοϑοῦο @> Եւ Ռեբեկալ ասաց գՅակոբ ցորդին իւր ցկրսեր. ագաւասիկ ես լուայ ի Հաւրէ: քում, վի խաւսեր ընդ Եսաւայ եղբաւր քո, եւ ասեր՝ բեր ինձ որս Հ Է. արա ինճ կերակուր եւ կերալց ե. աւրծնեցից զքեզ առաջի տեառն. արդ ալժճ որդեակ լուր Ճալնի իմում, եւ երթեալ ի խաշն՝ առ ինՃ անտի ուլս երկուս փափուկս եւ դեղեցիկս, եւ արարից ղնոսա կերակուր Հաւր քո որպես սիրե. եւ մուծցես առաջի Հաւր քո, զի կերիցե եւ աւրճնեսցե զքեզ Հայր քո լառաջ քան դմեռանել « Εἶπεν δὲ Ρεδέκκα πρὸς ᾿Ιακὼδ τὸν υἱὸν αὐτῆς τὸν νεώτερον' ἰδοὺ ἐγὼ ἤκουσα τοῦ πατρός σου λαλοῦντος πρὸς Ἡσαῦ τὸν ἀδελφόν σου καὶ λέγοντος" ἔνεγκέ μοι θήραν καὶ ποίησόν μοι ἐδέσματα καὶ φαγὼν εὐλογήσω σε ἐναντίον κυρίου. νῦν οὖν ἄκουσόν μου τέκνον τῆς φωνῆς καὶ πορευθεὶς εἰς τὰ πρόδατα λάδε μοι ἐκεῖθεν δύο ἐρίφους ἁπαλοὺς καὶ καλοὺς καὶ ποιήσω αὐτοὺς ἐδέσματα τῷ πατρί σου ὡς φιλεῖ καὶ εἰσοίσεις τῷ πατρί σου καὶ φάγεται, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ < σε > ὁ πατήρ σου πρὸ τοῦ ἀποθανεῖν αὐτόν ».
"Now Rebecca said to Jacob, her younger son: 'Behold, I have just heard your father speaking to Esau, your brother, and saying to him: "Bring me some of your game, and make me a dish, and, after having eaten, I will bless you before the Lord." Now, therefore, listen to my voice, my child, and, having gone (to the flock) of sheep, take me from there two little goats (36), tender and beautiful; and I will make a dish for your father, as he loves it, and you will bring it to your father, and he will eat of it; so that your father may bless you before he dies.'" "Now Rebecca said to Jacob, her younger son: 'Behold, I have just heard your father speaking to Esau, your brother, and saying to him: "Bring me some of your game, and make me a dish, and, after having eaten, I will bless you before the Lord." Now, therefore, listen to my voice, my child, and, having gone (to the flock) of sheep, take me from there two little goats (36), tender and beautiful; and I will make a dish for your father, as he loves it, and you will bring it to your father, and he will eat of it; so that your father may bless you before he dies.'"

On one hand, therefore, Rebecca, image-bearer of the Church, was already prefiguring (there) the future which was to be realized through her younger son. For she says to him: 'Having gone (to the flock) of sheep, take me from there two little goats, tender and beautiful.' Esau, for his part, is sent into the plain as living as a stranger (37) in the world; but Jacob (is sent) (to the flock) of sheep, so that what was said by the Lord might be fulfilled: 'I was not sent except to the lost sheep of the House of Israel.' On one hand, therefore, Rebecca, image-bearer of the Church, was already prefiguring (there) the future which was to be realized through her younger son. For she says to him: 'Having gone (to the flock) of sheep, take me from there two little goats, tender and beautiful.' Esau, for his part, is sent into the plain as living as a stranger (37) in the world; but Jacob (is sent) (to the flock) of sheep, so that what was said by the Lord might be fulfilled: 'I was not sent except to the lost sheep of the House of Israel.'
Τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν αὐτήν, μοι ἐκεῖθεν δύο ἐρίφους ἁπαλοὺς καὶ καλοὺς » ἔδειξεν τὰς δύο κλήσεις, τὰς διὰ τοῦ εὐαγγελίου γεγενημένας" ἔριφοι μὲν ὄντες ἀπ᾽ ἀρχῆς, ἐπειδὴ πάντες ἦμεν ἁμαρτωλοί, ἁπαλοὶ δὲ καὶ καλοὶ διὰ ὑπακοῆς γινόμεθα τῇ ἐν Χριστῷ πίστει δικαιούμενοι, μηκέτι ὡς ἔριφοι κρινόμενοι, ἀλλ᾽ ὡς πρόδατα εἰς « ὀσμὴν εὐωδίας » θυσίᾳ καθαρᾷ θεῷ προσφερόμενοι, < καὶ εἰς τροφὴν πνευματικὴν τῷ λόγῳ ἐξηρτισμένοι > ὃς διὰ τοῦ εὐαγγελίου τὰ ὅμοια ἐπιτελῶν ἔλεγεν τοῖς μαθηταῖς" « ἐγὼ βρῶσιν ἔχω φαγεῖν, ἣν ὑμεῖς οὐκ οἴδατε ». φῦ οδο ϑοῦ. On the other hand, from the fact that she said: 'Take me from there two little goats, tender and beautiful,' she indicated (thereby) the two calls which, in fact, are found to have been addressed by the Gospel. On one hand, being goats at the beginning (38), since we were all sinners, we become on the other hand, through obedience, tender and beautiful (39), justified by faith in Christ, no longer as condemned goats but, as sheep, in pure sacrifice, in an 'odor of sweetness' offered to God <and constituted spiritual food for the Word> (40) who, by means of the Gospel, giving the similitudes their full realization, said to his disciples: 'I have, myself, food to eat, which you do not know.'

Ὀούορδοδο >@- οδὸ Ὅωϑος» δῶσ Եւ զի ասաց ցնա. առ ինձ անտի երկուս ուլս փափ Հ ուկս եւ գեղեցիկս՝ եցոյց երկուս կոչմունսն ղաւհտարանաւն եղեալ. որք ի սկզբանե ուլքն հաք վասն պի ամենեքեան ճեղաւորք հաք, փափուկք եւ զեղեցիկք այժմ լենիմք լսելով, Քրիստոսի Հաւատովք արդարացեալ՝ ոչ եւս աաուգետեւ իբրեւ ուլք Գամարիմք, ալլ իբրեւ ոչխարք Հոտ անոյշ եւ զո սուրը՝ աստուծոյ մատչիմք, եւ ի կերակուր Հոգեւոր բանին ճաստատեալք, որ եւ լաւետարանին նմանադոլնսն կատարեր, ասելով ցաշակերտսն՝ եթե ես կերակուր ունիճ, զոր դուք ոչ դիտեք ։ Τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν αὐτήν, «λάδε μοι ἐκεῖθεν δύο ἐρίφους ἁπαλοὺς καὶ καλοὺς » ἔδειξεν τὰς δύο κλήσεις, τὰς διὰ τοῦ εὐαγγελίου γεγενημένας" ἔριφοι μὲν ὄντες ἀπ᾽ ἀρχῆς, ἐπειδὴ πάντες ἦμεν ἁμαρτωλοί, ἁπαλοὶ δὲ καὶ καλοὶ διὰ ὑπακοῆς γινόμεθα τῇ ἐν Χριστῷ πίστει δικαιούμενοι, μηκέτι ὡς ἔριφοι κρινόμενοι, ἀλλ᾽ ὡς πρόδατα εἰς « ὀσμὴν εὐωδίας » θυσίᾳ καθαρᾷ θεῷ προσφερόμενοι, < καὶ εἰς τροφὴν πνευματικὴν τῷ λόγῳ ἐξηρτισμένοι > ὃς διὰ τοῦ εὐαγγελίου τὰ ὅμοια ἐπιτελῶν ἔλεγεν τοῖς μαθηταῖς" « ἐγὼ βρῶσιν ἔχω φαγεῖν, ἣν ὑμεῖς οὐκ οἴδατε ». φῦ ὥωϑρς» οδο T b ογοῤοδῦο
ββχοῦδο > ϑησδορῶδο. σγοῤοδδο ®> ϑωησδορῶ > ϑοϑοῶον δῶσ ογοβοῦ-- ϑηθορῦθος» οδωδοῶδο > δυϑωϑῶςοοον» ϑοϑοῶον δοδεϑώδοβρδοδοον. οδο Ὀοῦορδοδο ϑοῦὺ >@- ὥωϑος» δῶσ Եւ զի ասաց ցնա. առ. ինճ անտի երկուս ուլս փափ Հ ուկս » եւ գեղեցիկս՝ եցոլց վերկուս .կոչմունսն զաւետարանաւն եղեալ. որք ի սկզբանե ուլքն նաք վասն զի ամենեքեան ճեղաւորք հաք, փափուկք եւ դեղեցիկք այժմ լինիմք լսելով, Քրիստոսի Տաւատովք արդարացեալ՝ ոչ եւս աաուֆետեւ իբրեւ ուլք Գամարիմք, ալլ իբրեւ ոչխարք Հոտ անոյշ եւ ἡπῷ սուրը՝ աստուծոյ մատչիմք, եւ ի կերակուր Հոգեւոր բանին ֆաստատեալք, որ եւ աւետարանին նմանագոլնսն կատարեր, ասելով ցաշակերտսն՝ եթե ես կերակուր πεῖ μὴ, զոր դուք ոչ դիտեք ։ V. «Εἶπεν δὲ Ἰακὼδ πρὸς 'Ρεδέκκαν τὴν μητέρα αὐτοῦ’ ἔστιν ἀδελφός μου ἀνὴρ δασύς, ἐγὼ δὲ ἀνὴρ λεῖος. μήποτε ψηλαφήσῃ με ὁ πατήρ μου καὶ ἔσομαι ἐναντίον αὐτοῦ ὡς κατα- φρονῶν καὶ ἐπάξω ἐπ᾽ ἐμαυτὸν κατάραν καὶ οὐκ εὐλογίαν.» πῶς σαφῶς ἐνθάδε τὸ ἴδιον εὐλαδὲς ὁ Ἰακὼδ ἐπέδειξεν. μαθὼν γὰρ τὸ γεγενημένον ὑπὸ τοῦ Χάμ, ὃς ἰδὼν γυμνωθέντα τὸν Νῶε κατεγέλασεν τὴν γύμνωσιν τοῦ πατρὸς αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐπεσπάσατο ἐφ᾽ ἑαυτὸν τὴν κατάραν, > g J ὥρορδοῦ δοσγοῶδοῳυ ὁ ὅϑοα ϑοδιορῶ φῦ ὃ θοῶς δοῦλα >

[p. 17] {#po.27.p17}
trying to deceive his own father and to fall under a curse. Furthermore, what is said (there) by him was also true, namely: 'Esau my brother, he is a hairy man,' that is to say, a sinner, 'but I am, myself, a smooth man,' by which is shown (the character) that the flesh of the Lord has of being without stain and without sin. But since it was not necessary that the mysteries, on another (than the Lord? Jacob?), be accomplished (45), (Rebecca) said to him (46): 'Upon me (be) your curse, my child (47); only be docile to my voice, and go, bring me...'

But Jacob said to Rebecca his mother: 'My brother is (41) a hairy man, and I, a smooth man. (I fear) that my father may come to feel me, and I will appear (42) in his eyes to despise him, and I will draw upon myself a curse and not a blessing.' How (43) clearly here Jacob has indeed shown his own piety! For, instructed in what had happened through the fault of Ham, who, having seen Noah naked, had mocked the nakedness of his father, and had drawn the curse upon himself, he took great care not to risk himself also (44) passing for someone

E. γὰρ ἔστιν ἐπιγνῶναι τὸ εἰρη μένον ὑπὸ τῆς 'Ρεδέκκας πάλαι, νῦν ἐπὶ τὴν ἐκ- κλησίαν πεπληρωμένον. τὸ γὰρ « ἐπ᾽ ἐμὲ ἡ κατάρα σου τέκνον », τοῦτο δηλοῖ, ὅπερ νῦν τινες βλασφημοῦντες τὴν ἐκκλησίαν ὀνειδίζουσιν ὡς σεδομένην τὸν ἐσταυρωμένον, κατάραν ἡμῖν καὶ ὄνειδος κατὰ ταῦτα ἐπιφέροντες. τὸ γὰρ πάθος τοῦ κυρίου τοῖς μὲν ἀπίστοις νομίζεται χατάρα, τοῖς δὲ πιστεύουσιν « ζωὴ καὶ εἰρήνη ». καὶ γὰρ ὁ ἀπόστολος « Χριστὸς ἡμᾶς ἐξηγόρασεν ἐκ τῆς κατάρας τοῦ νόμου, γενόμενος ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν κατάρα», ὅπερ ἐπεπλέρωσεν ὁ > ὁῶν ὥρδηροοῦ ϑορῶ βοδοουβοῶ,

For (49) the Passion of the Lord is, on one side, for the infidels, reputed a curse, on the other, for the faithful 'life and peace.' Now Isaac said to him (to Jacob): 'Who are you?' He said: 'I am your son, your firstborn Esau' (55). It is not because he is ignorant that the prophet questions Jacob; for he contemplates in their spiritual signification the events. Joshua, son of Nun, questioning the Gibeonites, was not indeed ignorant of what they had schemed in their deceit, but [knowingly] he pardoned, so that the Gentiles, coming to repentance and believing in Joshua and in Jesus, might be saved. Thus, therefore, here too (in Genesis), one must understand (56).

This signifies that (57) the Word, he who is in Jacob prefiguring the mysteries, also became the voice of the prophets, making himself in them see the future in advance (58). He (Isaac) said to him therefore: 'Approach me, (my) child, and kiss me.' And, having approached, he (Jacob) kissed him. And he (Isaac) smelled the odor of his garments and blessed him and said... And, saying this, the prophet clearly reveals in advance that none among men will offer the Father a holy mouth, except, and alone, the <son> (58bis) firstborn, born of the Virgin. 'I am,' says indeed (Jacob), 'your son, the firstborn.' Immediately, therefore, the prophet imposed his blessing upon him and said: 'Behold the odor of the garments of my son, <like> (59) the odor of a fertile field which the Lord has blessed! And may God give you, of the dew of heaven and of the fat of the earth, abundance of grain and wine! And may the <Gentiles> be enslaved to you, <and may> the princes <adore you> (60)! And be the lord of your brother, and may the sons of your father adore you! He who curses you, may he be cursed! He who blesses you, may he be blessed!'

Եւ ասէ ցնա. ճերձեաց առ իս, եւ Հաճբուրեա զիս` որդեակ ։ Եւ ճերձեցեալ՝ Հաճբուրեաց դնա. եւ Հոտոտեցաւ ի ճանդերձից նորա. եւ աւրգնեաց ղնա, եւ ասաց Ես գալս լալտնապես լառաքագոլն ակնարկե մարգարեն, եթե ոչ ոք ի ճարդկանե սուրբ բերան ճատուսցէ աւր, բայց միալն անդրանիկն, որ ի կուսէն Տնաւ որդի, քանզի ասե. ես եմ որդի քո անդրանիկ « Եւ անդեն եւ անդ լարեաց զաւրգնութիւնն ճարգարեն, եւ ասաց. ածաւասիկ Հոտ Գանդերձից որդւոյ իմո իբրեւ զգատ անդոյ լիոլ, զոր աւրճնեաց տեր. եւ տացե քեզ աստուած ի ցաւղոչ երկնից եւ ի պարարտութենե երկի, բազմութիւն ցորենոյ եւ գինւոյ. եւ Տառալեսցեն քեզ ազգք, եւ երկիր պագցեն քեզ` Աշխանք, եւ լեր տեր եղբաւը բում եւ երկիր պազցեն βίᾳ որդիք Հաւր քո « Որ անիՏանե զքել՝ անիծեալ, եւ որ աւրճնե գքել` աւրգնեալ եղեցի » Φησὶν οὖν πρὸς αὐτόν" « ἔγγισόν μοι, τέκνον, καὶ φίλησόν με. καὶ ἐγγίσας ἐφίλη- σεν αὐτόν. καὶ ὠσφράνθη τὴν ὀσμὴν τῶν ἱματίων αὐτοῦ, καὶ ηὐλόγησεν αὐτὸν καὶ εἶπεν ». καὶ τοῦτο σαφῶς προμηνύων ὁ προφήτης, ὡς οὐδεὶς ἀνθρώπων στόμα ἅγιον προσοίσει πατρὶ ἢ μόνος ὁ πρωτότοκος τῆς παρθένου γενόμενος < υἱός >. « ἐγὼ γάρ φησιν εἰμὶ ὁ υἱός σου, ὁ πρωτότοκος ». παραυτίκα γοῦν ἐπήνεγκεν τὴν εὐλογίαν ὁ προφήτης καὶ λέγει" « ἰδοὺ ὀσμὴ τῶν ἱματίων τοῦ υἱοῦ μου < ὡς > ὀσμὴ ἀγροῦ πλήρους, ὃν εὐλόγησε κύριος" καὶ δῴη σοι ὁ θεὸς ἀπὸ τῆς δρόσου τοῦ οὐρανοῦ καὶ ἀπὸ τῆς πιότητος τῆς γῆς πλῆθος σίτου καὶ οἴνου" καὶ δουλευσάτωσάν σοι .« ἔθνη, καὶ προσκυνήσουσίν σοι > ἄρχοντες, καὶ γίνου κύριος τοῦ ἀδελφοῦ σου, καὶ προσχυνήσουσίν σε οἱ υἱοὶ τοῦ πατρός σου. ὁ καταρώμενός σε ἐπικα- τάρατος, ὁ δὲ εὐλογῶν σε εὐλογημένος ». ϑιγϑυ-ϑοροδοῶδον» @G φροδοδοῦο > ϑηῶϑῃ δϑοδὺ Արդ եթե կարծե ոք զաւրծնունիւնս վալս ի Ցակոբ եղեալ, մոլորի, քանգի ոչինչ լալացանե եղեւ ի Ցակոբալ, զե գտանի լառաջ ի ճիջագետս քսան ամ ծառալեալ, ἔτη δουλεύων τῷ Λάβαν, ἔπειτα αὐτὸς τὸν ἴδιον ἀδελφὸν ᾿Ησαῦ προσκυνῶν καὶ διὰ τῶν δώρων τὸ τούτου πρόσωπον ἐξιλασκόμενος, μετὰ ταῦτα πάλιν εἰς Αἴγυπτον κατερχόμενος, ἵνα μὴ σὺν τοῖς τέκνοις λιμῷ διαφθαρῇ. ἐπὶ τίνα οὖν πληροῦται τὸ εἰρημένον" « ἰδοὺ ὀσμὴ τῶν ἱματίων τοῦ υἱοῦ μου’ ὡς ὀσμὴ ἀγροῦ πλήρης, ὃν εὐλόγησεν κύριος»; ἐπ᾽ οὐδένα ἄλλον, εἰ μὴ ἐπὶ τὸν Χριστόν, τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ θεοῦ. ἀγρὸς γάρ ἐστιν ὁ κόσμος. ὀσμὴ δὲ τῶν ἱματίων αὐτοῦ εἰσιν πάντες οἱ πιστεύοντες εἰς αὐτόν. ὡς ὁ ἀπόστολος λέγει" « ὅτι Χριστοῦ εὐωδία ἐσμὲν ἐν τοῖς σωζομένοις καὶ ἐν τοῖς ἀπολλυμένοις, οἷς μὲν γὰρ ὀσμὴ θανάτου εἰς θάνατον, οἷς δὲ ὀσμὴ ζωῆς εἰς ζωήν ».
favor before the face of the latter; after that, again (62) (he is found) descending into Egypt, so as not to perish from famine with his children. Upon whom, then, is what is said there fulfilled: "Behold, the smell of my son's garments is as the smell of a fruitful field which the Lord has blessed"? Upon no one other than Christ, the Son of God. For the field is the world. And the smell of his garments are all those who believe in Him, as the Apostle says: "We are the good odor of Christ among those who are being saved (63) and among those who are perishing; to the latter indeed an odor of death, for death; to the former, an odor of life, for life (64). If, therefore, anyone truly believes that this blessing was realized in Jacob, he is in error. For [none of these things] happened to Jacob (61). And the smell of his garments are all those who believe in Him, as the Apostle says: "We are the good odor of Christ among those who are being saved (63) and among those who are perishing; to the latter indeed an odor of death, for death; to the former, an odor of life, for life (64).


[p. 22] {#po.27.p22}
ἔτη δουλεύων τῷ Λάβαν, ἔπειτα αὐτὸς τὸν ἴδιον ἀδελφὸν Ἡσαῦ προσκυνῶν καὶ διὰ τῶν δώρων τὸ τούτου πρόσωπον ἐξιλασκόμενος, μετὰ ταῦτα πάλιν εἰς Αἴγυπτον κατερχόμενος, ἵνα μὴ σὺν τοῖς τέκνοις λιμῷ διαφθαρῇ. ἐπὶ τίνα οὖν πληροῦται τὸ εἰρημένον" « ἰδοὺ ὀσμὴ τῶν ἱματίων τοῦ υἱοῦ μου’ ὧς ὀσμὴ ἀγροῦ πλήρης, ὃν εὐλόγησεν κύριος»; ἐπ᾽ οὐδένα ἄλλον, εἰ μὴ ἐπὶ τὸν Χριστόν, τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ θεοῦ. ἀγρὸς γάρ ἐστιν ὁ κόσμος. ὀσμὴ δὲ τῶν ἱματίων αὐτοῦ εἰσιν πάντες οἱ πιστεύοντες εἰς αὐτόν. ὡς ὁ ἀπόστολος λέγει" « ὅτι Χριστοῦ εὐωδία ἐσμὲν ἐν τοῖς σωζομένοις καὶ ἐν τοῖς ἀπολλυμένοις, οἷς μὲν γὰρ ὀσμὴ θανάτου εἰς θάνατον, οἷς δὲ ὀσμὴ ζωῆς εἰς ζωήν ». οὐ ®> ϑοϑοοδώϑοςυ f οδο. δ᾽ g0- ὠχρροφοϑοδ, ὁ ὥωϑοφρο ϑοσγοῶϑοςυ Τ © Q> οδωῶρδοοο Ղաբանալ։ Եւ դարձեալ իւր եղբաւրն Եսաւալ էրկիր եպագ, եւ պատարադաւք զերեսս նորա ազ աչեայ ε Եւ լետ ալտրիկ նզւպտոս եջ, վե ճի Հանդերձ որդւովքն ի-ըովք ի սովոչ ապականիցի ε Եւ μὰ կատարի ասացեալն. ագաւասիկ Հոտ Տանդերձից որդւոլ իմոլ՝ իբըեւ զՏոտ անդոլ լեոլ, զոր աւրգնեաց տեր: Ոչ որ` ալլ ի Քրիստոս լորդին աստուճոլ Անդ Է աշխարգս, ոտ Հանդերձից նորա են ամենեքեան, որ ի նա Տաւատան, որպես առաքեալ ասե` եթե Քրիստոսի Հոտ անոյշ եճք ի կենդանիս եւ ի կորուսեալս. ոմանց Հոտ ի մագուանե ի մագ, եւ ոճանց ոտ կենդանութենէ ի կեանս « Z’. Ալ գասելե՝ Թէ տացէ քեզ աստուած ի ցաւղոլ երկնից եւ ի պարարտուքենէ երկըի, բազճութիւն յորենոլ ն գինւոլ, լալտնապես ճառիւն բանն, որ երկնից Էջ էբըեւ զցաւղ նշանակե: Նւ երկիր` զճարճինն, որ ի կուսեն ընկալաւ ε Բազճութիւն ցորենոյ եւ գինւոլ ասացեալ՝ ղսուրբան եցոյց, շտեմարան Հ իբրն ցորեան > ժողովեալ եւ Հոդւովն իբըեւ դինեաւ արդարացեալս
As for this saying: "May God give you of the dew of heaven and of the fatness of the earth, abundance of grain and wine," very clearly, by the term (employed), he designated the Word, who descended from heaven like dew; and, by the earth, the flesh which he assumed from the Virgin; and, by saying: "Abundance of grain and wine," he showed the saints gathered like grain (65) in a storehouse and, thanks to the Spirit, [justified as if by wine] (66). As for this saying: "May God give you of the dew of heaven and of the fatness of the earth, abundance of grain and wine," very clearly, by the term (employed), he designated the Word, who descended from heaven like dew; and, by the earth, the flesh which he assumed from the Virgin; and, by saying: "Abundance of grain and wine," he showed the saints gathered like grain (65) in a storehouse and, thanks to the Spirit, [justified as if by wine] (66).

Τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν’ « δουλουσάτωσάν σοι ἔθνη καὶ προσκυνήσουσίν σοι ἄρχοντες » < τοῦτο καὶ μεχρὶ νῦν γίνεται" τίνι σήμερον τὰ ἔθνη πιστεύοντα δουλεύουσι καὶ τίνα προσκυνοῦσιν οἱ τῆς ἐκκλησίας ἄρχοντες > ἀλλ᾽ ἢ τὸν Χριστόν, δι’ οὗ ὀνόματος καὶ σώζονται. ὡς καὶ διὰ Ἡσαΐου αὐτὸς ὁ λόγος προαναφωνῶν « τοῖς δὲ δουλεύουσίν μοι κληθήσεται ὄνομα καινόν, ὃ εὐλογηθήσεται ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς" εὐλογήσουσιν γὰρ τὸν θεὸν τὸν ἀληθινὸν < καὶ οἱ ὁμνύοντες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς ὁμοῦνται τὸν θεὸν τὸν ἀληθινόν > ». καὶ πάλιν λέγει" «ἰδοὺ οἱ δουλεύοντές μοι φάγονται, ὑμεῖς δὲ πεινάσετε᾽ ἰδοὺ οἱ δουλεύοντές μοι πίονται, ὑμεῖς δὲ διψήσετε᾽ ἰδοὺ οἱ δουλεύοντές μοι ἀγαλλιάσονται ἐν εὐφροσύνῃ, ὑμεῖς δὲ αἰσχυνθήσεσθε, ἀπὸ συντριδῆς πνεύματος ὀλολύξετε ». ®> ὥσϑ)0). σγή)5. ὁϑωδοδςοῦδ > ογομεδοῦὃ
As for this saying: "May the Gentiles be enslaved to you! And may princes worship you" <this, even until this hour, is being realized. Who, today, do the Gentiles, having become believers, serve? And whom do the princes of the Church worship?> (67), if not Christ, by whose name they are also saved? As also, through Isaiah, the Word himself, announcing it in advance, says: "Those who serve me shall be called by a new name, which shall be blessed upon the earth. For they shall bless the true God; <and those who swear by the heavens, shall swear (henceforth) by the true God> (68). And again he says: "Behold, those who serve me shall eat; but you, you shall be hungry; behold, those who serve me shall drink; but you, you shall be thirsty; behold, those who serve me shall exult with joy; but you, you shall be in shame; in the tearing of your spirit you shall howl"

Եսալեաւ նքե բանն գոչե՝ ասելով. որ Տառալենն ինճ, կոչեսցի անուն նոր, որ աւրգնեսցի ի վերալ երկրի, քանզի աւրճնեսցեն ղաստուած ճշմարիտ: Եւ որթ երդնուն չերկինս, երդեուն աստուած Ճշճարիտ ε Եւ դարձեալ ասէ՝ խե ագաւասիկ որ Տառալենն ինճ, կերիցեն, եւ դուբ՝ բաղցիջիք. ագաւասիկ որ ծառալենն ինձ, արբցեն, եւ դութ՝ Տարաւ- եսջիք. ագաւասիկ որ Տա ՀՀ ռալենն ինձ 5», ցնծասցեն ուրախութեամբ, եւ դութ՝ ամաչեսջեր եւ ի բեկմանե ոգւոչ աղաղակեսջիք »

Then he adds, saying: "Be lord of your brother; and may the sons of your father worship you." In truth, regarding Jacob, both are false: no one worshipped him and he was not lord of his brother Esau; but, quite the contrary, he fled from him in fear, and, first, worshipped him seven times. What, therefore, is

said there has its fulfillment in the Savior. For of those who are regarded as being, according to the flesh, his brothers, he became Lord and Master (69), so that, by them, as King, he might be worshipped. It is for this that he says: "Whoever curses you, let him be cursed! But whoever blesses you, let him be blessed!" Then he adds, saying: "Be lord of your brother; and may the sons of your father worship you." In truth, regarding Jacob, both are false: no one worshipped him and he was not lord of his brother Esau; but, quite the contrary, he fled from him in fear, and, first, worshipped him seven times. Without any doubt, the dish of Esau designates the worship of the people under the law, in that (this people) boasted and considered itself to be justified by circumcision. They offered (to God) the Gentile proselytes as food, lacking food themselves, not being able to touch the heavenly bread (71). p. < H> Εἴπεν οὖν Ἡσαῦ" « ἀναστήτω ὁ πατήρ μου καὶ φαγέτω τῆς θήρας τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ με ἡ ψυχή σου ». πῶς ὑπερήφανον τὸ ῥῆμα καὶ ἐπηρμένον τῇ καρδίᾳ" καὶ γὰρ ἕως σήμερον οἱ ἐκ περιτομῆς ὁμοίως καυχῶνται, ὡς μόνοι δεδικαιωμένοι. ὁ δὲ μακάριος Ἰακὼδ τὸ εὐλαδὲς καὶ ταπεινόφρον προσλαδόμενος ἔλεγεν τῷ πατρί" « ἐποίησα καθ᾽ ἃ ἐλάλησάς μοι»" ὁ δὲ ᾿Ησαῦ οὐχ οὕτως, ἀλλὰ « ἀναστήτω ὁ πατήρ μου καὶ φαγέτω τῆς θήρας τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ με ἡ ψυχή σου ». ὁ δὲ πρὸς αὐτὸν ἔφη" «τίς εἴ σύ; ὁ δὲ εἴπεν' ἐγώ εἰμι ὁ υἱός σου, ὁ πρωτότοκος, ᾿Ησαῦ. ἐξέστη δὲ ᾿Ισαὰκ ἔκστασιν. μεγάλην σφόδρα καὶ εἴπεν᾽ τίς οὖν ὁ θηρεύσας μοι θήραν καὶ εἰσενέγκας μοι, καὶ ἔφαγον ἀπὸ πάντων πρὸ τοῦ σε ἐλθεῖν καὶ ηὐλόγησα αὐτόν, καὶ εὐλογημένος ἔσται ». πῶς ἐγρηγόρησεν ἐνθάδε τὸ πνεῦμα τοῦ προφήτου. ἐπειδὴ γὰρ ἐν τῇ εὐλογίᾳ προσομιλῶν τῷ Ἰακὼδ εἴπεν᾽ « ὁ εὐλογῶν σε εὐλογημένος" ὁ δὲ καταρώμενός σε ἐπικατάρατος», διὰ τοῦτο ἐπεσφαλίσατο ἐκ δευτέ- ρου καὶ ἐπεσφράγισεν τὴν πρώτην εὐλογίαν εἰπών' « ηὐλόγησα αὐτὸν καὶ εὐλογημένος ἔσται »ν. ἦν γὰρ τὸ γεγονὸς ὑπὸ τοῦ Ἰακὼδ μυστήριον οἰκονομίας εἰς Χριστὸν προτυπού- μενον, ὃς εὐλογημένος ὑπὸ τοῦ πατρὸς γεννηθεὶς εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας ἐγένετο εὐλογημένος. βοϑω > ΕΝ δυςοοῶοῦυ δὺδ δϑοδοῦσα >
Esau therefore said: "Let my father arise and eat of his son's game; so that your soul may bless me." What pride in these words! And what arrogance in this heart! And, indeed, until today, those of the circumcision boast similarly, thinking they alone are justified. The blessed Jacob, for his part, joining to it piety and humility of spirit, said to his father: "I have done one by one (the things) that you told me (to do)." Esau, for his part, nothing of the sort, but: "Let my father arise and eat of his son's game; so that your soul may bless me." Now (Isaac) said to him: "Who are you?" (Esau) said: "I am your son, your firstborn, Esau." Then, beside himself, Isaac was greatly, very strongly astonished, and said: "Who is it then who went to hunt game for me and brought it to me; and I ate of everything before you came; and I blessed him, and blessed he shall be." How the spirit of the prophet remained awake here! Because, indeed, in his blessing, addressing Jacob, he had said: "Whoever blesses you, let him be blessed! But whoever curses you, let him be cursed!" For this reason, he confirmed a second time and sealed the first blessing by saying: "I have blessed him, and blessed he shall be." What had indeed happened by the act of Jacob was a mystery of (divine) economy in view of Christ prefigured (by Jacob), (of Christ) who, blessed by the Father, once born (temporal birth), was blessed for the ages.

Θώϑῆφρο δοῦ ϑωδοςν ἰδ δοογδὸδ < H> Εἴπεν οὖν Ἡσαῦ’ « ἀναστήτω ὁ πατήρ μου καὶ φαγέτω τῆς θήρας τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ με ἡ ψυχή σου ». πῶς ὑπερήφανον τὸ ῥῆμα καὶ ἐπηρμένον τῇ καρδίᾳ" καὶ γὰρ ἕως σήμερον οἱ ἐκ περιτομῆς ὁμοίως καυχῶνται, ὡς μόνοι δεδικαιωμένοι. ὁ δὲ μακάριος Ἰακὼθδ τὸ εὐλαδὲς καὶ ταπεινόφρον προσλαθόμενος ἔλεγεν τῷ πατρί" « ἐποίησα καθ᾽ ἃ ἐλάλησάς μοι»" ὁ δὲ Ἡσαῦ οὐχ οὕτως, ἀλλὰ « ἀναστήτω ὁ πατήρ μου καὶ φαγέτω τῆς θήρας τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ, ὅπως εὐλογήσῃ με ἣ ψυχή σου ». ὁ δὲ πρὸς αὐτὸν ἔφη" «τίς σύ; ὁ δὲ εἴπεν" ἐγώ εἰμι υἱός σου, ὁ πρωτότοκος, ᾿Ησαῦ. ἐξέστη δὲ ᾿Ισαὰκ ἔκστασιν. μεγάλην σφόδρα καὶ εἴπεν᾽ τίς οὖν ὁ θηρεύσας μοι θήραν καὶ εἰσενέγκας μοι, καὶ ἔφαγον ἀπὸ πάντων πρὸ τοῦ σε ἐλθεῖν καὶ ηὐλόγησα αὐτόν, καὶ εὐλογημένος ἔσται ». πῶς ἐγρηγόρησεν ἐνθάδε τὸ πνεῦμα τοῦ προφήτου. ἐπειδὴ γὰρ ἐν τῇ εὐλογίᾳ προσομιλῶν τῷ Ἰακὼδ εἴπεν' « ὁ εὐλογῶν σε εὐλογημένος" ὁ δὲ καταρώμενός σε ἐπικατάρατος», διὰ τοῦτο ἐπεσφαλίσατο ἐκ δευτέ- ρου καὶ ἐπεσφράγισεν τὴν πρώτην εὐλογίαν εἰπών' « ηὐλόγησα αὐτὸν καὶ εὐλογημένος ἔσται». ἦν γὰρ τὸ γεγονὸς ὑπὸ τοῦ Ἰακὼθδ μυστήριον οἰκονομίας εἰς Χριστὸν προτυπού- μενον, ὃς εὐλογημένος ὑπὸ τοῦ πατρὸς γεννηθεὶς εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας ἐγένετο εὐλογημένος. δ0 ϑοϑοο βῆϑαι > δοφροῶοῦο δὺδ ϑοῦ» ϑρδοῦδα ον δ Ὀκηφ»ϑοδ ϑοδϑοδ. ὁϑδοῥοδοδοῦοδο @> δῶφροῦδο >> φυϑοϑῳογθὺ ὥωϑος» οδοςοοῦῦ > βἢδωδρῦγδ οδοῦο δοδϑοζθογᾳρῃ δος δῥόδῶο οὐδωὸ > θοῶςοδυ > 3s ϑοϑοῦο δῶν δῶρ 36g». p. Τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν τὴν γραφήν" « ἐξέστη δὲ Ἰσαὰκ < ἔκστασιν μεγάλην σφόδρα », τοῦτ᾽ ἔστιν" ἐθαύμασεν Ἰσαὰκ > ἐπὶ τῷ γεγονότι, προορῶν τὰ > ἔθνη διὰ τοῦ νεωτέρου υἱοῦ < ὑπὸ >> τοῦ θεοῦ μέλλοντα εὐλογεῖσθαι καὶ εἰς τὴν διαθήκην τῆς ἐπαγγελίας τῶν πατέρων χωρεῖν. διὰ τοῦτο γὰρ καὶ Ἰακὼδ τικτόμενος καὶ ἔσχατος ἐκ κοιλίας μητρὸς ἐκπορευόμενος τὴν πτέρναν ἐκράτει τοῦ Ἡσαῦ" ἤμελλεν γὰρ ὁ τοῖς ἴχνεσιν τῶν προφητῶν χατακολουθῶν ἔσχατος λαὸς λήψεσθαι τὰ πρωτοτόκια εἰς καινὴν διαθήκην πρῶτος εὑρισκόμενος. δῶοδγω οὐ» οδο.
As for what the Scripture says: "Then, beside himself, Isaac <was greatly, very strongly astonished; this is the equivalent of: "Isaac was seized with admiration> (72)" regarding what had happened, seeing in advance that, the Gentiles, it was through the younger son that, <by> (73) God, they were to be blessed and to access the testament of the Promise made to the Fathers. It is for this reason, indeed, that Jacob even, at the moment when he was being born and coming out last from his mother's womb, was clutching the heel of Esau. It is that, indeed, following closely in the tracks of the prophets, the last people was to seize the birthright, the first that one finds in the New Testament (74).

Ալ զի ասացն գիր` եխե գիացաւ Իսագակ Հիացումն Յեծ լոլժ, այս Է եթե զար- մացաւ ի վերալ եղելոցն, առաջ տեսանելով ղազգս տղայ որդւովն աստուծո Հան- դերձեալ աւրծնիլ, եւ լուխտ խոստճան Հարցն մտանել ։ Քանի եւ վասն ալսորիկ Ցակոբ՝ ի ծնանելե եւ. լետոլ ի մաւրն լոլովալնե՝ առաջ զալ, գարշապարեն ուներ զեսաւալ. դի Հանդերձեալ Էր երկրորդ ժողովուրդն զճետոց ճարգարեիցն երթալ եւ առնուլ դանդրանկութիւնն, եւ ի նոր կտակարանս առաջին գտանել p. Τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν τὴν γραφήν" « ἐξέστη ἑκσθασιν μεγάλην σφόδρα », τοῦτ᾽ ἔστιν" ἐθαύμασεν Ἰσαὰκ > ἐπὶ τῷ γεγονότι, προορῶν τὰ > ἔθνη διὰ τοῦ νεωτέρου υἱοῦ < ὑπὸ >> τοῦ θεοῦ μέλλοντα εὐλογεῖσθαι καὶ εἰς τὴν διαθήκην τῆς ἐπαγγελίας τῶν πατέρων χωρεῖν. διὰ τοῦτο γὰρ καὶ Ἰακὼδ τικτόμενος καὶ ἔσχατος ἐκ κοιλίας μητρὸς ἐκπορευόμενος τὴν πτέρναν ἐκράτει τοῦ Ἡσαῦ" ἤμελλεν γὰρ ὁ τοῖς ἴχνεσιν τῶν προφητῶν χατακολουθῶν ἔσχατος λαὸς λήψεσθαι τὰ πρωτοτόκια εἰς καινὴν διαθήκην πρῶτος εὑρισκόμενος. »ῶοδγω οδο. δοογοϑοφο

Ալ վի ասացն գիր` եթե գիացաւ Իսագակ Գիացումն Ցեծ լոլժ, այս Է՝ եթե զար- մացաւ ի վերալ եղելոցն, առաջ տեսանելով զազգս տղայ որդւովն աստուծո Հան- դերձեալ աւրծնիլ, եւ լուխտ խոստճան Հարցն մտանել ։ Քանի եւ վասն այսորիկ Ցակոբ՝ ի ծնանելն եւ. լետոլ ի մաւրն որովալնե առաջ գալ, զգարշապարեն ուներ զեսաւալ. դի Հանդերձեալ Էր երկրորդ ժողովուրդն վգետոց ճարգարեիցն երթալ եւ առնուլ ղանդրանկութիւնն, եւ ի նոր կտակարանս առաջին գտանել « Ἐγένετο ἡνίκα ἤκουσεν τὰ ῥήματα ᾿Ισαὰκ τοῦ πατρὸς αὐτοῦ, ἀνεδόησεν φωνὴν μεγάλην καὶ: πικρὰν σφόδρα καὶ εἴπεν' εὐλόγησον δὴ κἀμὲ πάτερ. εἶπεν δὲ αὐτῷ" ἐλθὼν ὁ ἀδελφός σου μετὰ δόλου ἔλαδεν τὴν εὐλογίαν σου».
"It happened, when Esau had heard the words of Isaac his father, that he cried out very loudly, a great and vehemently bitter cry, and said: "Bless me then, me also, father!" (Isaac) said to him: "Your brother, having come with guile, has taken your blessing."

Οθοϑ og0:. բանն աստուծոյ մարմնանալ եւ կերպարանս ծառայի առնուլ, զի նովաւ անծանաւթ Տնեալ՝ ընկալցի ղաւրգնութիւնն ի Հաւրէ, տացէ եւ մեզ որք ի նա Հաւատամք Τὸ οὖν εἰπεῖν αὐτῷ" « ἐλθὼν < ὁ ἀδελφός > σου μετὰ δόλου < ἔλαδεν τὴν εὐλογίαν φῦ οδο δοϑωοβοδοδὺ T ἃ Եւ զասելն նորա. նենգութեամբ՝ նշանակե խործրդեամբ, քանզի Հանդերձեալ Եր σημαίνει μοστικῶς, ὅτι ὃ λόγον τοῦ θεοῦ, σαρκωθεὶς μορφὴν δούλου λᾶμ- θάνειν, ἵνα δι᾿ αὐτοῦ ἄγνωστος γεννηθεὶς ἀναδέξηται τὴν εὐλογίαν παρὰ τοῦ πατρός, μετα- διδοὺς καὶ ἡμῖν τοῖς ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν πιστεύουσιν. Ὁ δὲ ἀκούσας ἔφη" « δικαίως ἐκλήθη τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ Ἰακώδ' ἐπτέρνισεν γάρ ἤδη δεύτερον τοῦτο᾽ τά τε πρωτοτόκιά μου εἴληφεν καὶ νῦν ἔλαδεν τὴν εὐλογίαν μου. καὶ ἐνεκότει Ἡσαῦ τῷ Ἰακώθ, καὶ εἶπεν ἐν τῇ διανοίᾳ αὐτοῦ πότε ἐγγίσωσιν αἱ ἡμέραι τοῦ πένθους τοῦ πατρός μου, ἵνα ἀποκτείνω Ἰακὼδ τὸν ἀδελφόν μου; » > ϑοῶσγφροδς᾽ ὁθῶςοῦ ϑοῦο οὐβδωδ. αὔχοῦ ϑοϑοδεςϑὺ ϑοϑορα δοῶϑϑωρὄδα @> > > > ϑοϑοῦὸ ϑωδϑόφοο οὐδοχῦ ὅϑοα եւ Եսաւայ լուեալ փիրաւի՝ ասաց` կոչեցաւ անուն նորա` Ցակոբ. ագաւասիկ ալա երկիցս անգամ խաբեաց զիս. առ զանդրանկուԹիւնսն իճ եւ ալժճ առ. զաւրգնութիւնսն իճ ։ Եւ ոխապագծ Եր Եսաւ Յակոբայ, եւ ասաց ի սրտի իւրում. Էրբ ճերձեսցին աւուրք մագուան Հաւր իմոլ, զի սպանից զծակոբ եղբայ իճ: Ὁ δὲ ἀκούσας ἔφη" « δικαίως ἐκλήθη τὸ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ Ἰακώδ' ἐπτέρνισεν γάρ ἤδη δεύτερον τοῦτο’ τά τε πρωτοτόχιά μου εἴληφεν καὶ νῦν ἔλαδεν τὴν εὐλογίαν μου. καὶ ἐνεκότει Ἡσαῦ τῷ ᾿ἸΙακώδ, καὶ εἶπεν ἐν τῇ διανοίᾳ αὐτοῦ λέγων" πότε ἐγγίσωσιν αἱ ἡμέραι τοῦ πένθους τοῦ πατρός μου, ἵνα ἀποχτείνω Ἰακὼδ τὸν ἀδελφόν μου; » > ϑοῶσγφροῦς ὁθῶςοῦ ϑοῦο οὐβδωδ.
Now Esau, once he had heard this, said: "Quite rightly he was called by the name of Jacob! For he had already (78) supplanted me! This time it is the second! My birthright he has already taken! And now he comes to take my blessing!" "And Esau remained deeply irritated against Jacob. And he said, saying it in his heart: "When will the days approach (79) when I will mourn for my father? So that I may kill Jacob, my brother!" Now Esau, once he had heard this, said: "Quite rightly he was called by the name of Jacob! For he had already (78) supplanted me! This time it is the second! My birthright he has already taken! And now he comes to take my blessing!" "And Esau remained deeply irritated against Jacob. Էրբ մերձեսցին աւուրք ճագուան աւր իճոլ, ղի սպանից զՅակոբ զեղբայր իճ: Πῶς σαφῶς ἐνθάδε προανεφώνησεν ᾿ἣ γραφὴ < διὰ τὰ συμδησόμενα τὰ συναφῆ᾽ > καὶ γὰρ ὁ λόγος πατὴρ τοῦ λαοῦ ἐδείκνυτο, ὃς ἐπ᾽ ἐσχάτων διὰ τῆς σαρκὸς ἐπὶ τὸ πάθος ἤγγισεν. ὡς Μωσῆς ὀνειδίζών τὸν λαὸν λέγει" « ταῦτα κυρίῳ ἀνταποδίδοτε, οὐκ αὐτός σου πατήρ; » διέστειλεν οὖν ἡ γραφή, ἵνα νοήσωμεν τὸ εἰρημένον. τὸ γὰρ εἰπεῖν τὸν ᾿Ησαῦ" « πότε ἐγγίσωσιν αἱ ἡμέραι τοῦ πέν θους τοῦ πατρός μου; » περὶ τῶν ἡμερῶν τοῦ πάσχα διηγεῖται, ἐν αἷς παρήει ὁ λόγος κηρύσσων αὐτοῖς τὴν βασιλείαν. ϑοσ)δῶθο >> ογή)οῦ ϑορῶ
How manifestly here the Scripture has predicted <through Esau the future events that happened together>! And, indeed, the Word "Father of the people" was shown there, (Word) who, in the last times, thanks to the flesh (which he had taken) approached, until touching it, the Passion (80). As Moses, reproaching the people, says: "Is this what you render to the Lord? Is He not your Father?" The Scripture has therefore distinguished so that we might well understand what was said there. For the fact that Esau says: "When will the days approach when I will mourn for my father?" is explained by the days of the Passover,
during which the Word [was approaching], preaching to them the Kingdom (81). But that Esau says: "So that I may kill Jacob, my brother," it is so that (thereby) the man himself might be shown (in the Word), the man who, according to the flesh, was begotten of Jacob, and, by him (Esau = the Jewish people) nailed to the wood. How manifestly here the Scripture has predicted <through Esau the future events that happened together>! And, indeed, the Word "Father of the people" was shown there, (Word) who, in the last times, thanks to the flesh (which he had taken) approached, until touching it, the Passion (80). As Moses, reproaching the people, says: "Is this what you render to the Lord? Is He not your Father?" The Scripture has therefore distinguished so that we might well understand what was said there. For the fact that Esau says: "When will the days approach when I will mourn for my father?" is explained by the days of the Passover,

թե սպանից զծակոր զեղբալը իմ, զե երեւեսցի մարդն, որ ըստ մարժնոլն ի Ցակոբայ ծնեալ, եւ ի նոցանեն ի վերալ փայտի բեւեռեցաւ ε " Πῶς σαφῶς ἐνθάδε προανεφώνησεν ᾿ἣ γραφὴ < διὰ Ἡσαῦ τὰ συμδησόμενα τὰ συναφῆ᾽ > καὶ γὰρ ὁ λόγος πατὴρ τοῦ λαοῦ ἐδείκνυτο, ὃς ἐπ᾽ ἐσχάτων διὰ τῆς σαρκὸς ἐπὶ τὸ πάθος ἤγγισεν. ὡς Μωσῆς ὀνειδίζών τὸν λαὸν λέγει" « ταῦτα κυρίῳ ἀνταποδίδοτε, οὐκ αὐτός σου πατήρ; » διέστειλεν οὖν ἡ γραφή, ἵνα νοήσωμεν τὸ εἰρημένον. τὸ γὰρ εἰπεῖν τὸν ᾿Ησαῦ' « πότε ἐγγίσωσιν αἱ ἡμέραι τοῦ πέν θους τοῦ πατρός μου; » περὶ τῶν ἡμερῶν τοῦ πάσχα διηγεῖται, ἐν αἷς παρήει ὁ λόγος κηρύσσων αὐτοῖς τὴν βασιλείαν. ϑοσ)δῶβο >> ογήη)θῦ ϑοδδον ϑορῶ ϑοϑὸςο δοϑωβδοοοδωςου. ώϑοφρο οδο ϑοϑῳωδώϑος J δ. ὁδῶν ϑωσδϑῦον. > ϑοϑοῦν ϑοδενωχδδο» Որպես լալտնապես աստ լառաջագոլն ասացին դիրք Եսաւաւ պլինելոցն ՀՀ զլարե լոցն քանզի բանն Հաւր ժողովրդեանն երեւեր, որ լետոլ ապա եւ ճարծնովն ի չարչարանսն մերձեցաւ « Ասաց եւ Մովսէս՝ նախատելով զժողովուրդն. զայդ տեառն գատուցանեք. ոչ սա ինքն Է Հայր քո: Որոշեաց դիրն, դի իմասցուք զասացեալն ։ Եւ ղասելն Եսաւալ՝ Թե երբ մերձեսցին աւուրք մածուան Հաւր իմոլ, վասն աւուրցն զատկի պատճե, լոր δρᾶ Էր բանն քարոզելով նոցա զարքալութիւն Այլ` որ ասեն՝ πὸ δὲ ἵνα Ἰακὼβ τὸν ἀδελφόν », ἕνα δειχθῇ αὐτὸς & ἄνθρωπος, ὁ σάρχα ἐκ τοῦ Ἰακὼδ γεννηθεὶς καὶ ὑπ᾽ αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ ξύλου προσηλωθείς. ϑοϑοογὺ ϑοϑοῶον Τ' a υνδορδοῦδω οὐχ ὕωφροαχ ϑοογοῶδηςο ϑαιβόφου οὐδωῦ δῦσα ὀοϑωβῦςονδ οδο. ϑοροθδο ὁωῶβοον οὐδωδοῖ ϑωδοφν > δὺδ ὕρφου «οοδϑωφοφορδε)φο:. թե սպանից զծակոր զեղբալը իժ, զե երեւեսցի մարդն, որ ըստ մարժնոլն ի Ցակոբայ ծնեալ, եւ ի նոցանեն ի վերալ փայտի բեւեռեցաւ ε " ΙΧ. Esau (82) therefore said to him (to Isaac): "Have you not, besides, left a blessing for me, father?" Indeed, so that the whole fullness of the divinity might be found to dwell bodily in Christ, the latter (83) (Isaac) said to him: "If I have made him your Lord, and made all his brothers his servants, I have (at the same time) consolidated upon him (alone) the rights to the grain and to the oil; from then on, for you, what can I do, child? (84)" Esau (82) therefore said to him (to Isaac): "Have you not, besides, left a blessing for me, father?" Indeed, so that the whole fullness of the divinity might be found to dwell bodily in Christ, the latter (83) (Isaac) said to him: "If I have made him your Lord, and made all his brothers his servants, I have (at the same time) consolidated upon him (alone) the rights to the grain and to the oil; from then on, for you, what can I do, child? (84)"

"Before the breaking (85) of Isaac, Esau cried out a great cry and wept." The breaking of Isaac, therefore, is the pity which, to the very bowels, moved the Word before the transgression (86) of the people. And, indeed, the Savior, praying for them, said: "Father (87), forgive them, for they do not know what they are doing." "Before the breaking (85) of Isaac, Esau cried out a great cry and wept." The breaking of Isaac, therefore, is the pity which, to the very bowels, moved the Word before the transgression (86) of the people. And, indeed, the Savior, praying for them, said: "Father (87), forgive them, for they do not know what they are doing."

X. ἀλλ᾽ ἐπειδὴ καιρὸς οὐκ ἦν, προφητεύων Ἰσαὰκ τὰ μέλλοντα φησὶν πρὸς αὐτόν’ «ἰδοὺ ἀπὸ τῆς πιότητος < γῆς ἔσται ἡ κατοίκησίς σου καὶ ἀπὸ τῆς δρόσου τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἄνωθεν καὶ ἐπὶ τῇ μαχαίρᾳ σου ζήσει καὶ τῷ ἀδελφῷ σου δουλεύσεις" ἔσται δὲ ἡνίκα ἂν καθέλῃς καὶ ἐκλύσῃς τὸν ζυγὸν ἀπὸ τοῦ τραχήλου σου ». τὰ μὲν εἰρημένα ὑπὸ τοῦ μακαρίου ᾿Ιδαὰκ πότερον εὐλογίας ἔχει δύναμιν ἢἣ προφητείας; χρὴ γὰρ νοεῖν τὰ προκείμενα. ἰδού « ἀπὸ τῆς πιότητος τῆς γῆς ἔσται « ἡ > κατοίκησίς σου, καὶ ἀπὸ τῆς δρόσου τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἄνωθεν ». συνέθη γὰρ χατοικῆσαι τὸν λαὸν ἐν τῇ γῇ τῶν Χαναναίων, < ἣν > διένειμεν αὐτοῖς Ἰησοῦς ὁ τοῦ Ναυῇ. δῇ δῶν ονῦρ8). ὥωδῃςοῖλο
That, then, was the blessing that Esau was asking to receive at that time. But because it was not the moment, Isaac, prophesying the future, said to him: "Behold, of the fatness of the earth shall your dwelling be, and of the dew that descends from heaven (94). And by your sword you shall live, and your brother you shall serve. Yet a time will come when you shall shake off and loosen the yoke from off your neck." Indeed, does what the blessed Isaac says there have the value of a blessing or of a prophecy? For one must well understand what precedes it. Behold, he says: "Of the fatness of the earth shall your dwelling be, and of the dew that descends from heaven." It happened, in fact, that the people settled in the land of the Canaanites which Joshua, son of Nave, divided among them. And that Isaac says: "and of the

dew that descends from heaven" is because the prophets, like a cloud, left them soaked with dew (95) after having made known to them (96) the oracles of God. And that he says: "By your sword you shall live" is because the people never ceased to be in aggressive and defensive war against the peoples established all around them, as the Scriptures themselves show. And what he says: "And your brother you shall serve" marks the epoch that still lasts now, (the epoch) when the Savior was present, having come to visit his own brothers according to the flesh, (the Savior) whom the prophet (here) advises, after one has become (97) docile, to serve. That is why he said: "Yet a time will come when you shall shake off and loosen the yoke from off your neck." What yoke if not the yoke that lies in the Law? So that they might no longer live as slaves under the yoke of the Law, but that, as free men believing in the Gospel, they might now still be saved (98). That, then, was the blessing that Esau was asking to receive at that time. But because it was not the moment, Isaac, prophesying the future, said to him: "Behold, of the fatness of the earth shall your dwelling be, and of the dew that descends from heaven (94). And by your sword you shall live, and your brother you shall serve. Yet a time will come when you shall shake off and loosen the yoke from off your neck." Indeed, does what the blessed Isaac says there have the value of a blessing or of a prophecy? For one must well understand what precedes it. Behold, he says: "Of the fatness of the earth shall your dwelling be, and of the dew that descends from heaven." τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν « καὶ ἀπὸ τῆς ϑρόσου τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἄνωθεν », ὅτι οἱ προφῆται νεφέλης ἐδρόσιζον αὐτοὺς γνωρίσαντες αὐτοῖς τὰ τοῦ θεοῦ λόγια. τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν « ἐπὶ τῇ μαχαίρᾳ σου ζήσει », ὅτι ἀεὶ ὁ λαὸς οὐκ ἐπαύσατο πολεμῶν καὶ πολεμούμενος πρὸς τὰ κύκλωθεν ἔθνη κατοικοῦντα, καθὼς αὐταὶ αἱ γραφαὶ δηλοῦσιν. τὸ δὲ εἰπεῖν « καὶ τῷ ἀδελφῷ σου δουλεύσεις » σημαίνει τὸν νῦν ἐνεστῶτα καιρόν, ἐν ᾧ παρῆν ὁ σωτὴρ ἐπισκεψάμενος τοὺς ἰδίους κατὰ σάρκα ἀδελφούς, ᾧ παραινεῖ ὁ προφήτης ὑπήκοον γενάμενον δουλεύειν. διὰ τοῦτο ἔφη « ἔσται δὲ ἡνίκα ἂν καθέλῃς καὶ ἐκλύσῃς τὸν ζυγὸν ἀπὸ τοῦ τραχήλου σου ». ποῖον ζυγόν, ἀλλ᾽ ἢ τὸν ἐν τῷ νόμῳ κείμενον; ἵνα μηκέτι νῦν δοῦλοι ὑπὸ ζυγὸν νόμου πολιτεύωνται, ἀλλὰ ἐλεύθεροι τῷ εὐαγγελίῳ πιστεύοντες καὶ νῦν σωθῆναι δύνωνται. ϑοδοοθοῶδηδοςθονῦο ὕρφου ϑοογοῦϑοςυ ϑοῶσςοοῦ δῶν > δῶϑθωφοοςο Θωδοςφοῦο φοοδρ»οῶρδιης» ὀγβηηφδοῦφδ τοῦ ϑοογοῦϑεςο ϑϑοδο δα θοϑο 5b. ϑοῦφρω- ὅρος» J > ϑοογοῶϑος > ϑοδοῦο »6>0g ὥδωογ» ϑωδοῦο βοογοῶβο δοδογοβοδεγσ)φοοῦογοοῦο δοδοῶρδοδο θυ σὺ R. վի ճարգարեքն իբրեւ ղամպս առաքեին զցաւղն, քարողելով նոցա զաստուծոլ բանսն ։ իսկ զասելե՝ եթե սրով քով կեցջեր, զի Հանապալ ժողովուրդն ոչ դաղարեաց պատերաճեալ եւ պատերալճիլ ի շուրջ ազգացն, որ բնակեալ եին, որպես եւ գիրք ցուցանեն ։ Իսկ ասելե` Թե եղբաւր քում Տառաչեսջիր, նշանակե զալժմու ժամանակս, յորում մերձ եր փրկիչն, այց առնելպվ իւրոց եղբարցն, որ ըստ մարժնոլ ε Որում մարգարեն խրատ տալ՝ Տն աղանդս եղեալ Ֆառալել Վասն այնորիկ ասաց` եթե եղեցի, որժամ լուծցյես եւ ընկեսցես զլուծն ի պարանոցե բում ։ Զր γι δ᾽ եթե ոչ որ աւրինացն կայր ի վերաչ, ղի մի եւս իբըեւ ծառայք ընդ լծաւ աւրինացն վարիցին, ալլ իբրեւ աղատք աւետարան ՀՀ ի ճաւատացեալ` հ ալժճ կեալ կարասցեն ։ Αὕτη οὖν ἡ εὐλογία, ἣν ἐζήτει λαδεῖν ὁ τότε. « ἔσται δὲ ἡνίκα ἂν καθέλῃς καὶ ἐκλύσῃς τὸν ζυγὸν ἀπὸ τοῦ τραχήλου σου ». ποῖον ζυγόν, ἀλλ᾽ ἢ τὸν ἐν τῷ νόμῳ κείμενον; ἵνα μηκέτι νῦν δοῦλοι ὑπὸ ζυγὸν νόμου πολιτεύωνται, ἀλλὰ ἐλεύθεροι τῷ εὐαγγελίῳ πιστεύοντες καὶ νῦν σωθῆναι δύνωνται.

Προμηνύσαντες οὖν τὰς ὑπὸ τοῦ Ἰσαὰκ εἰρημένας εὐλογίας, παρέλθωμεν καὶ ἐπὶ τὰς ὑπ᾽ αὐτοῦ τοῦ ᾿Ιακὼδ γεγενημένας πρὸς τοὺς υἱοὺς εὐλογίας. φησὶν γὰρ ἡ γραφὴ οὕτως. « ἰδὼν δὲ Ἰσραὴλ τοὺς υἱοὺς Ἰωσὴφ τίνες εἰσὶν οὗτοι ὁ δὲ εἴπεν᾽ υἱοί μου εἰσίν, οὺς δέδωκέν μοι ὁ θεὸς ἐνταῦθα. καὶ εἴπεν᾽ προσάγαγέ μοι αὐτούς, ἵνα εὐλογήσω αὐτούς. οἱ δὲ ὀφθαλμοὶ Ἰακὼδ ἐδαρύνθησαν ἀπὸ τοῦ γήρως, καὶ οὐκ ἠδυνήθησαν τοῦ βλέπειν. καὶ ἤγγισεν αὐτοὺς πρὸς αὐτόν, καὶ ἐφίλησεν αὐτοὺς καὶ περιέλαθεν. καὶ εἶπεν Ἰσραὴλ πρὸς Ἰωσήφ" ἰδοὺ τοῦ προσώπου σου οὐκ ἐστερήθην καὶ ἰδοὺ ἔδειξέν μοι ὁ θεὸς καὶ τὸ σπέρμα σου. καὶ > δῶ ϑωϑοςοθον οὐδωδοῦσγο ϑδογθὺ
After having first given these indications on the blessings pronounced by Isaac, let us come also to the blessings (102) which are found in fact to have been, by Jacob himself, addressed to his sons. Thus, in fact, says the Scripture: "Now Israel, at the sight of the sons of Joseph, said: 'Who are these?' (Joseph) said: 'These are my sons, whom God has given me here.' And (Jacob) said: 'Bring (103) them to me, that I may bless them.' Now the eyes of Jacob had become heavy because of old age, and could (104) no longer see well. And (Joseph) brought them near to him, and he kissed them and held them embraced (105). And Israel said to Joseph: 'Behold, I have not been deprived of (seeing) your face, and behold, God has shown me even your lineage!' And Joseph withdrew them gently <from between

the knees of (Jacob), and they worshipped him face to the ground. And Joseph having taken> (106) his two sons, Ephraim to his right, therefore to the left of Israel, and Manasseh to his left, therefore to the right of Israel, brought them near to (Jacob). Now Israel, having extended his right hand, placed it on the head of Ephraim—now this one was the younger—and his left hand on the head of Manasseh. Having thus crossed his hands, he blessed them and said: "O God, before whom my fathers, Abraham and Isaac, were pleasing, O God who has been nourishing me from my youth until the day we are in, O Angel who delivered me from all my evils, bless (107) these little children, and upon them shall remain imposed my name and the name of my fathers, Abraham and Isaac, and may they multiply into a great multitude on the earth!" After having first given these indications on the blessings pronounced by Isaac, let us come also to the blessings (102) which are found in fact to have been, by Jacob himself, addressed to his sons. Thus, in fact, says the Scripture: "Now Israel, at the sight of the sons of Joseph, said: 'Who are these?' (Joseph) said: 'These are my sons, whom God has given me here.' And (Jacob) said: 'Bring (103) them to me, that I may bless them.' Now the eyes of Jacob had become heavy because of old age, and could (104) no longer see well. And (Joseph) brought them near to him, and he kissed them and held them embraced (105). Եւ եան զնոսա Ցովսեփ ի ծնգաց նորա, եւ երկիր պագին նմա ի վերայ երեսաց իւր- եանց չերկիր Առեալ Յովսեփայ ղերկուս որդիսն իւր` վԵվըեժ ընդ աջմէ, ի Ճախմե Իսըալեղի, եւ վՍՄանասե ի ձախճե, ընդ աջճե Իսրալեղի, մերձեցոլց զնոսա առ նա « Եւ ἄηβωι Իսրալեղի զաջ ձեռն իւր` եղ ի վերալ գլխոլն Եփրեմի, որ Եր կրսեր, եւ վճախն ի վերալ դլխոլն Մանասեի, լճեռս զմիճեամբք արկեալ. աւրծնեաց զնոսա, եւ ասաց. աստուած որո առաջի Գաճոլ եղեն Հարքն իճ Աբրագամ եւ Իսագակ, աստուած՝ որ կերակրեցեր զիս ի մանկութենէ մինչեւ ցաաաւր, Տրշտակգ՝ որ փրկեցեր վիս լամենալն չարեաց իճոց, աւրգնեա զմանկունսս զայս. եւ կոչեսցի ի վերայ սոցա անուն իմ եւ անուն Հարցն իճոց՝ Աբրածամու եւ Իսագակալ. եւ բաղճասցին a I τοῦ Ἰσαὰκ εἰρημένας εὐλογίας, παρενθοβονκαὶ ἐπὶ τὰς ὑπ᾽ αὐτοῦ τοῦ ᾿Ιακὼδ γεγενημένας πρὸς τοὺς υἱοὺς εὐλογίας. φησὶν γὰρ ἡ γραφὴ οὕτως. « ἰδὼν δὲ Ἰσραὴλ τοὺς υἱοὺς Ἰωσὴφ τίνες εἰσὶν οὗτοι ὁ δὲ εἴπεν' υἱοί μου εἰσίν, οὺς δέδωκέν μοι ὁ θεὸς ἐνταῦθα. καὶ εἴπεν᾽ προσάγαγέ μοι αὐτούς, ἵνα εὐλογήσω αὐτούς. οἱ δὲ ὀφθαλμοὶ Ἰακὼδ ἐδαρύνθησαν ἀπὸ τοῦ γήρως, καὶ οὐκ ἠδυνήθησαν τοῦ βλέπειν. καὶ ἤγγισεν αὐτοὺς πρὸς αὐτόν, καὶ ἐφίλησεν αὐτοὺς καὶ περιέλαθεν. καὶ εἶπεν Ἰσραὴλ πρὸς Ἰωσήφ' ἰδοὺ τοῦ προσώπου σου οὐκ ἐστερήθην καὶ ἰδοὺ ἔδειξέν μοι ὁ θεὸς καὶ τὸ σπέρμα σου. καὶ > δῶ οδο οὐδωδοῦσγο ϑδογθὸ οὐ ϑοσγοῶϑοςο > Θωϑοφοῦο ϑωδρδο ὃ 3f> > ϑωιϑοοῦφροδοον οδοῦο > φοοϑϑοδοῦς)ς» > 3g@ @> ϑοοοῦφοδο οδοῦο @> ὁῦς»δο ϑωοδῦδο > οϑδωῶν-).ὦ > 3G» οὐῶσθᾳοδϑοῦ ե δοθοῦν T a σῶν @> ϑοβεγοῦο τοϑοῶσνϑοῦ րդ ցուցեալ զասացեալ աւրֆնութիւնսն Իսագակալ, անցցուք եւոր ի Ցակոբալն եղեւ՝ առ. որդիսն իւր աւրգնունիւնս, քանզի ասէ դիր ալսպես. տեսեալ ` ասե, Իսրայ- եղի զորդիսն Յուսիփալ, ասաց. նլք են սոքա Եւ նա ասաց. որդիք իմ են, զորս ետ ինձ աստուած աստ » Եւ ասաց. ածեք առ. իս զդոսա, պի աւրճնեցից վդոսա ։ Եւ աչք Ցակոբալ Տանրատես եին ի Ֆերութենէ, եւ ոչ կարեր տեսանել « Եւ ճերձեցոլց զնոսա առ. նա, եւ ի դիրկս՝իւը առնալ՝ Տաճբուրեաց զնոսա Եւ ասաց Սսրալեղ ցՅուսեփ. ածաւասիկ ի տեպենե երեսաց "ὲ ալլ եցոլց աստուած եւ վզաւակ քո: p. ἐξήγαγεν αὐτοὺς Ἰωσὴφ < ἀπὸ τῶν γονάτων αὐτοῦ, καὶ προσεκύνησαν αὐτῷ ἐπὶ πρόσωπον ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς" λαδὼν δὲ Ἰωσὴφ > τοὺς δύο υἱοὺς αὐτοῦ, τόν τε 'Εφραὶμ ἐν δεξιᾷ, ἐξ ἀριστε- ρῶν δὲ τοῦ Ἰσραήλ, καὶ τὸν Μανασσῆν ἐν τῇ ἀριστερᾷ, ἐκ δεξιῶν δὲ Ἰσραήλ, ἤγγισεν αὐτοὺς πρὸς αὐτόν. ἐκτείνας δὲ Ἰσραὴλ τὴν χεῖρα τὴν δεξιάν, ἐπέθαλεν ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν Ἐφραίμ, οὗτος δὲ ἦν ὁ νεώτερος, καὶ τὴν ἀριστερὰν ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν Μανασσῇ, ἐναλλάξας τὰς χεῖρας ηὐλόγησεν αὐτοὺς καὶ εἶπεν" ὁ θεός, ᾧ εὐηρέστησαν οἱ πατέρες μου ἐναντίον αὐτοῦ, ᾽᾿Αδραὰμ καὶ Ἰσαάκ, ὁ θεὸς ὁ τρέφων με ἐκ νεότητός μου ἕως τῆς σήμερον ἡμέρας, ὁ ἄγγελος, ὁ ῥυόμενός με ἐκ πάντων τῶν κακῶν μου, εὐλόγησον τὰ παιδία ταῦτα, καὶ ἐπιτεθήσεται τὸ ὄνομά μου ἐπ᾽ αὐτούς: καὶ τὸ ὄνομα τῶν πατέρων μου ᾿Αθραὰμ καὶ ᾿Ισαὰκ καὶ πληθυνθείησαν εἰς πλῆθος πολὺ ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς». the knees of (Jacob), and they worshipped him face to the ground. And Joseph having taken> (106) his two sons, Ephraim to his right, therefore to the left of Israel, and Manasseh to his left, therefore to the right of Israel, brought them near to (Jacob). Now Israel, having extended his right hand, placed it on the head of Ephraim—now this one was the younger—and his left hand on the head of Manasseh. Having thus crossed his hands, he blessed them and said: "O God, before whom my fathers, Abraham and Isaac, were pleasing, O God who has been nourishing me from my youth until the day we are in, O Angel who delivered me from all my evils, bless (107) these little children, and upon them shall remain imposed my name and the name of my fathers, Abraham and Isaac, and may they multiply into a great multitude on the earth!"

By saying this, the prophet has openly made us see a very great mystery. For in the fact of having crossed his hands and (thus) made the younger of the sons of Joseph, I mean Ephraim, pass to his right, but Manasseh, the elder, to his left, he has, through them, shown that there existed two calls and two peoples;

Θωῦδῳ» δοῶδϑωο ϑοῶρὕρδοο». > φῦ Զայս ասացեալ ճարգարեին՝ մեծ խորգուրդ եցոլց մեզ: Եւ զճիճեաճբքն արկանել վզճեռսն, եւ վկրտսեր որդին Յովսեփար՝ զնփըեմ՝ յաջ անցուցանել, եւ ἡ Մանասե, որ Էր անդրանիկ, ի Ճախակողմն, եցոլց նոքաւք երկուս կոչճունս եւ երկուս ժողո- p. καὶ δύο λαοὺς γινομένους" καὶ τὸν μὲν νεώτερον διὰ πίστεως εἰς δεξιὰν τοῦ Χριστοῦ εὑρισκό- μενον, τὸν δὲ πρεσθύτερον λαόν, τὸν ἐν τῷ νόμῳ καυχώμενον, εἰς ἀριστερὰν μετατιθέμενον. ὅθεν μὴ νοήσας ὁ Ἰωσὴφ πνευματικῶς τὸ γινόμενον ὑπὸ τοῦ προφήτου, οἰόμενος πλα- νᾶσθαι τὸν πατέρα, ἀντελάθετο αὐτοῦ καὶ εἴπεν᾽ « οὐχ οὕτως, πάτερ, αὐτὸς γάρ ἐστιν ὁ πρωτό- τοχος, ἐπίθες τὴν δεξιάν σου ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν αὐτοῦ. ὁ δὲ εἴπεν᾽ οἶδα, τέκνον, καὶ αὐτὸς ἔσται μέγας καὶ αὐτὸς ὑψωθήσεται, ἀλλ᾽ ὁ ἀδελφὸς αὐτοῦ ὁ νεώτερος μείζων αὐτοῦ ἔσται »" « καὶ ἔθηκεν τὸν ᾿Εφραὶμ ἔμπροσθεν τοῦ Μανασσῆ ». ἀλλ᾽ ἐπειδὴ λεληθότως πολλὰ ἔγκειται ἐν ταῖς ἁγίαις γραφαῖς καὶ τοῦτο ἀφθόνως διηγησόμεθα, τί λέγων ὁ μακάριος Ἰακὼδ
and that the younger, he, thanks to faith, is found at the right of Christ, while the older people, those who glory in the Law, are, by mutation, placed at the left. Whence Joseph, not having understood (108) in its spiritual significance what was being done there by the prophet, thinking that his father was mistaken (109), stopped (110) him, and said: "Not so, father! For this one is the firstborn! Put your right (hand) on his head." But Jacob said: "I know, my child; he too shall be great, he too shall be exalted, but his brother, the younger, shall be greater than he." And he placed (111) Ephraim before Manasseh. Moreover, since there are, in the Holy Scriptures, many things (112) in a hidden state, this too we shall explain at length, namely what meaning the words have by which the blessed Jacob made the two sons of Joseph his own (113). The fact of saying, in effect: "the two sons born in Egypt before I came to you in Egypt, are mine; Ephraim and Manasseh, like Reuben and Simeon, shall be mine; as for the younger ones that you will beget (114) after them, they shall be yours," marks well that (that Jacob makes Ephraim and Manasseh his own). Since in fact Jacob had twelve sons, to whom precisely the twelve tribes owe their


[p. 48] {#po.27.p48}
ἰδιοποιήσατο τοὺς δύο υἱοὺς τοῦ Ἰωσήφ. τὸ γὰρ εἰπεῖν « οἱ δύο υἱοί, οἱ γενάμενοι ἐν Αἰγύπτῳ πρὸ τοῦ με ἐλθεῖν πρός σε εἰς Αἴγυπτον, ἐμοί εἰσιν, Ἐφραὶμ καὶ Μανασσῆς, ὡς Ῥουδὴμ καὶ Συμεὼν ἔσονταί τὰ δὲ ἔγγονα, ἃ γεννήσεις μετὰ ταῦτα, σοὶ ἔσονται », τοῦτο σημαίνει: ἐπειδὴ γὰρ δώδεκα ἦσαν υἱοὶ τοῦ Ἰακώδ, ἐξ ὥνπερ αἱ δώδεκα φυλαὶ γεγένηνται, p. ϑοῶχς» δοῶβηφρο 3g g>6.
existence, he divided the two sons of Joseph into two tribes; and the tribes were from then on to the number of thirteen, the tribe of Joseph having been divided between his two sons. And thus was not without having something to do (here), Paul, the Apostle (115). Chosen (116) from among the number of the tribes (Benjamin) he was counted thirteenth after the Apostles, and thus he was, to the Gentiles, as an Apostle, sent. By saying this, the prophet has openly made us see a very great mystery. For in the fact of having crossed his hands and (thus) made the younger of the sons of Joseph, I mean Ephraim, pass to his right, but Manasseh, the elder, to his left, he has, through them, shown that there existed two calls and two peoples;

վուրդս եղեալ, եւ գկրտսերն գաւատովք ընդ աջմէ Քրիստոսի գտեալ իսկ զանդրանիկ ժողովուրդն, որ լաւրենսն պանծացեալ Էր, ի Ճախակողծն եդեալ ։ Վասն որոլ չիճա- ցեալ Յովսեփայ գոդեպես գեղեալե ի ճարգարեեն՝ կարծձեաց՝ եթե ճոռացաւ Տալն, վասն որոչ կալեալ դճեռանե նորա` ասաց. ոչ ալդպեստ՝ Հայր. սա Ե անդրանիկ. դիր գաջ բո ի վերալ գլխոլ սորա Եւնա ասաց. գիտեճ՝ որդեակ` գիտեմ. եւ սա եղեցի ՑեՖ եւ բարձրասցի, ալ եղբա սորա կրտսեր մեծագոյն քան զսա եղիցի. եւ նատոլց վեփըեմ առաջի Մանասեի Ալ վասն զի աներեւոլ շատ ինչ կալ ի գիրս սուրբ, եւ զալ աննախանՃ պատճեսյուք, որով բանիւ սեփականեցոլց երանելին Ցակոբ զերկուս որդիսն Յովսեփայ: Քանզի ասաց նճա. երկու որդիբդ քո՝ որ եղեն քեզ` լեդւպտոս, մինչեւ եկեալ եի ես առ քեզ լեգւպտոս, իճ ԵՆ, Եփրեմ եւ Մանասե, որպես Ռուբեն եւ Սիճեռն եղեցին իճ ։ Եւ այ որդիք՝ որքան եւ Տնցիս ետ այսորիկ, ո եղիցին τ Եւ ալս նշանակե. վասն զի երկոտասան եին Յակոբու որդիք, որոց եր- ὅδο υἱοῦς τοῦ Ἰωσῆ εἰς βδόο φυλὰς μωᾷ ἐγένοντο τφολοῖ βεκοττρθῖς, πῆς φυλῆς Ἰωσὴφ εἰς τοὺς δύο υἱοὺς μερισθείσης" καὶ οὕτω οὐκ ἀργὸς ἦν Παῦλος ὁ ἀπόσ- τολος, τρισ καιδέκατος μετὰ τοὺς ἀποστόλους, ἐκλεγεὶς ἐκ τοῦ ἀριθμοῦ τῶν φυλῶν, κατελογίσθη καὶ οὕτως τοῖς ἔθνεσιν ἀπόστολος ἐπέμφθη. p. δογαῶϑοδ, δέβο ουδωδοῦδο. δὸδ δογαῶ- ϑοόδο ὅ5δο > © θοῦ ϑοοογροϑοόσῳ ϑρρῶσγοα > ϑωβοΐος δον δ ΡΟ p. կոտասան ազգքն եղեն, զերկուս որդիսն Յովսեփայ երկուս ազգս բաժանեաց. եւ եղեն ազգք երեքտասան, ազգին Մովսեփալ երկուս որդիս բաժանեալ ε Եւ այա ոչ ընդ վալը վասն վի Պաւղոս առաքեալ երերտասաներորդ լետ առաքելոցն ընտրեալ, ի իուռլ ազգացն գամարեցաւ, եւ առաքեալ աղգաց առաքեցաւ ὁ τῶν τρον, ἐδ ἀμ δ

ϑοϑοοδώϑοςο ὁϑοῦν Արգ սրբոց գրոց ամենեւին լմեր զմիտս Գանեալ, մարգարեին փորձելով, տեսցութ՝ լետ ալորիկ` դինչ ասէ « Ἐκάλεσεν δὲ Ἰακὼθ τοὺς υἱοὺς αὐτοῦ καὶ εἴπεν' συνάχθητε, ἵνα ἀπαγγείλω ὑμῖν, τί ἀπαντήσει ὑμῖν ἐπ’ ἐσχάτῳ τῶν ἡμερῶν᾽ ἀθροίσθητε καὶ ἀκούσατε, υἱοὶ Ἰακώθ, ἀκούσατε Ἰσραὴλ τοῦ πατρὸς ὑμῶν ». οὐ οὐδοῦ οὐ ϑοϑωϑοροῦ- (> ὁδὸ δ δ ϑῶ- δοῶδοον > ՝ կոչեաց` ասէ, Ծակոբ զորդիսն իւր, եւ ասաց. ժողովեցարուք, եւ պատճեցից Ճեզ զինչ պատածելոց իցե ձեզ ի լետին ժամանակի. ժողովեցարութ եւ լուարուք՝ որդիբ Ցակոբալ, լուարուք Իսրալեղի՝ Հաւր ձերում « Ἐκάλεσεν δὲ Ἰακὼθ τοὺς υἱοὺς αὐτοῦ καὶ εἴπεν' συνάχθητε, ἵνα ἀπαγγείλω ὑμῖν, τί ἀπαντήσει ὑμῖν ἐπ’ ἐσχάτῳ τῶν ἡμερῶν᾽ ἀθροίσθητε καὶ ἀκούσατε, υἱοὶ Ἰακώθ, ἀκούσατε Ἰσραὴλ τοῦ πατρὸς ὑμῶν».
"Now Jacob called his sons and said: 'Gather yourselves together, that I may announce to you what will happen to you in the last days. "Now Jacob called his sons and said: 'Gather yourselves together, that I may announce to you what will happen to you in the last days. Close the ranks; and listen, sons of Jacob, listen to Israel, your father.'"

For the blessing, it (118) concerns someone whom one blesses, while the prophecy is realized at the accomplishment of a certain fact. With these words of the Scripture: "All these are the twelve sons of Jacob; <and> (119) it is thus that their father spoke to them and that he blessed them; he blessed them each according to his blessing (120)," how will the (explanation) proposed above (121) concord: sometimes prophecies manifestly such, sometimes (prophecies) named blessings? (122) In this (explanation) it must be understood that it is in the very (things) expressed that lie both blessings and prophecies, in such a way that the blessings, they, fall upon Him who was born of Judah, and upon Him who is prefigured in Joseph, upon Him who, (descended) from Levi, is found to be Priest of the Father, while the prophecies (fall and remain) upon those who acted as adversaries and, for nothing, held the Son of God. Without any doubt when he says: "Gather yourselves together, that I may announce to you what will happen to you in the last days," that is prophecy and not blessing.

ճեզ ի ետին ժաման > ակի, ալս մարգարէութիւն Է եւ ոչ աւրգնութիւն Աւրնութիւն՝ լորժամ աւրգնի ոք, ի նա ասի Իսկ ճարգարեութիւն լինի ի Հ վերալ > զործոլ իրիք կատարելոլ ։ Եւ գրոց. ասացեալ՝ եթե ամենայն ալս որդիք Ծակոբալ երկոտասան, եւ ղայս խաւսեցաւ ընդ նոսա Հայրն նոցա, եւ աւրգնեաց դնոսա, իւրաքանչիւր ըստ աւրգնուխեան նոցա աւրծնեաց ղնոսա Եւ վիմրդ ճիաբանեսցի առաջակալն. երբ մարգարէութիւն ցուցանի, կամ ճրժաճ աւրգնուԹիւն անուանի ϑωμιᾶ պարտ Է իճանալ՝ եթե ի նոսին ճառսն եւ աւրծնութիւնք կան եւ ճարդգա- բեութիւնք, զի աւրճնութիւնքն եկեսցեն լալն՝ որ ի Յուղալեն ծնաւն, եւ որ Յովսեվաւն նշանակեցաւ, եւ որ ի Ղեւի քագանալ Հաւր գտաւ: խկ ճարգարեութիւնքն μείω որ պրնդդեմն գործեցին եւ զորդին աստուծո արծամարծեցին » Τὸ μὲν οὖν εἰπεῖν αὐτόν’ « συνάχθητε, ἵνα ἀπαγγείλω ὑμῖν τί ἀπαντήσει ὑμῖν ἐπ᾽ ἐσχάτῳ φῦ Θωϑοφοῦο οδο οὐ δοογοῶϑρφυ. ϑοϑωδϑοροῶδοον > ογβ)δ ὥσω ὁδο δῶν σγβεγοδτοῦ δ ϑοδοουθοῶδϑοὀοοοουδδὺ δῦ Նւ զասելն նորա` եթէ ժողովեցարուք, եւ պատճեցից ἅδη զինչ պա Հ տագելոց p. τῶν ἡμερῶν », προφητεία ἐστὶν τοῦτο, οὐκ εὐλογία < ἡ μὲν γὰρ εὐλογία > φέρεται ἐπί τινι εὐλογουμένῳ, ἡ δὲ προφητεία γίνεται ἐπὶ ἔργῳ τινὶ. τελουμένῳ. λεγούσης οὖν τῆς γραφῆς" « πάντες οὗτοι υἱοὶ Ἰακὼδ δώδεκα, < καὶ > ταῦτα ἐλάλησεν αὐτοῖς ὁ πατὴρ αὐτῶν καὶ εὐλόγησεν αὐτοὺς ἕκαστον, κατὰ τὴν εὐλογίαν αὐτοῦ ηὐλόγησεν αὐτούς », πῶς συμφωνήσει τὸ προχείμενον, ποτὲ μὲν προφητεῖαι δειχνύμεναι, ποτὲ δὲ εὐλογίαι ὀνομαζό- μεναι; ἐν τούτῳ δεῖ νοεῖν, ὅτι ἐν αὐτοῖς τοῖς ῥητοῖς καὶ εὐλογίαι κεῖνται καὶ προφητεῖαι, ἵνα αἱ μὲν εὐλογίαι καταντήσωσιν ἐπὶ τὸν ἐκ τοῦ Ἰούδα γενόμενον καὶ τὸν ἐν τῷ Ἰωσὴφ προτυπούμενον, τὸν ἐκ τοῦ Λευὶ ἱερέα τοῦ πατρὸς εὑρισκόμενον, αἱ δὲ προφητεῖαι ἐπὶ τοῖς τὰ ἐναντία πράξασιν καὶ τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ θεοῦ ἐξουδενήσασιν.
